Conclusion: 速度-质量权衡并非扩散模型缓存的固有属性，而是固定阈值策略的人为产物。通过动态识别关键步并差异化阈值，ACID在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升推理速度，为视频扩散模型的实用部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Video diffusion models produce high-quality generations but remain slow at inference due to their sequential denoising procedure. Caching-based acceleration methods address this by reusing intermediate model outputs: leading dynamic approaches such as TeaCache, EasyCache, and DiCache accumulate a drift signal and skip expensive model evaluations when accumulated drift stays below a fixed threshold τ. This threshold controls an apparent tradeoff - raising it yields faster generation at the cost of visual quality, while lowering it preserves quality but sacrifices speed. We show this tradeoff is not fundamental; it is an artifact of holding τ constant throughout denoising. We identify the existence of critical steps - timesteps where the drift signal changes rapidly - and show that applying a low threshold selectively at these steps while caching aggressively elsewhere recovers most of the quality of conservative caching at substantially higher inference speeds. Building on this insight, we propose ACID, a lightweight, training-free wrapper that monitors the rate of change of each method's existing drift signal to dynamically switch between a low and a high threshold. ACID is signal-agnostic and modular: it requires no retraining and plugs directly into existing dynamic caching methods without modifying their core mechanisms. Evaluated across three caching methods (TeaCache, EasyCache, DiCache) and three open-source video diffusion models (HunyuanVideo, Wan 2.1, CogVideoX), ACID consistently expands the Pareto frontier of visual quality versus inference speed beyond what any fixed threshold achieves. In particular, on TeaCache and HunyuanVideo, ACID achieves up to 2.16x speedup over the no-caching baseline, and up to 38% additional speedup over the conservative fixed-threshold baseline with negligible (<0.3 dB PSNR, <0.01 SSIM, <0.01 LPIPS) quality degradation.

</details>


### [35] [Implicit 4D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Motion with Large Inter-Frame Displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12362)
*Seung-gyeom Kim,Areum Kim,Yongjae Yoo,Sukmin Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPIN-4DGS 提出用时空位置隐式网络来预测4DGS中的高斯属性，替代传统的时间位移建模方式，解决了快速运动和大帧间位移下重建质量差的问题，在CMU Panoptic体育场景上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4DGS方法在快速运动和大帧间位移场景下表现不佳：高斯属性在训练中难以良好学习，快速移动物体常常从重建中丢失。核心问题在于传统方法建模时间位移（temporal displacements）的方式不足以应对剧烈的帧间变化。

Method: SPIN-4DGS引入时空位置隐式网络（Spatiotemporal Position Implicit Network），从显式采集的时空位置直接学习高斯属性，而非建模时间位移。为规避显式优化所有时空位置属性的巨大内存开销，采用轻量级前馈网络在基于光栅化的重建损失下进行预测。该网络在高斯体之间学习共享表示，有效捕捉时空一致性。

Result: 在CMU Panoptic数据集的挑战性体育场景上，SPIN-4DGS在大位移下持续实现更高保真度，PSNR和SSIM均有明显提升。在Basketball场景上，比最强基线D3DGS的PSNR高出+1.83。

Conclusion: 通过时空位置隐式网络预测高斯属性，SPIN-4DGS能够在快速大位移运动下实现稳定、高质量的4D高斯泼溅，为动态场景重建提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) methods often fail under fast motion with large inter-frame displacements, where Gaussian attributes are poorly learned during training, and fast-moving objects are often lost from the reconstruction. In this work, we introduce Spatiotemporal Position Implicit Network for 4DGS, coined SPIN-4DGS, which learns Gaussian attributes from explicitly collected spatiotemporal positions rather than modeling temporal displacements, thereby enabling more faithful splatting under fast motions with large inter-frame displacements. To avoid the heavy memory overhead of explicitly optimizing attributes across all spatiotemporal positions, we instead predict them with a lightweight feed-forward network trained under a rasterization-based reconstruction loss. Consequently, SPIN-4DGS learns shared representations across Gaussians, effectively capturing spatiotemporal consistency and enabling stable high-quality Gaussian splatting even under challenging motions. Across extensive experiments, SPIN-4DGS consistently achieves higher fidelity under large displacements, with clear improvements in PSNR and SSIM on challenging sports scenes from the CMU Panoptic dataset. For example, SPIN-4DGS notably outperforms the strongest baseline, D3DGS, by achieving +1.83 higher PSNR on the Basketball scene.

</details>


### [36] [Lost in Visual Translation: A VLM-Assisted Perceptual-Semantic Coherence Framework for EEG-to-Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12364)
*Sukriti Tiwari,BHVSP Subrahmanyam,Nidhi Goyal,Sai Amrit Patnaik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有EEG-to-image评估指标(SSIM, LPIPS, CLIP)无法区分视觉保真度与语义可恢复性，本文提出BCI-Coherence Score (BCS)，通过VLM共识蒸馏实现感知-语义联合评估，与人类判断高度一致(Cohen's κ=0.882)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图(EEG)重建图像通常模糊、失真且细节匮乏，传统像素指标(SSIM/LPIPS)与语义一致性几乎零相关，表示学习指标(CLIP)则混淆感知与语义错误。现有评估范式未能区分"视觉上不像但语义可恢复"与"视觉逼真但语义错误"两种截然不同的失败模式，亟需一个BCI场景专用的评估框架。

Method: 研究者在ATM、ENIGMA、BrainVis、DreamDiffusion四个模型生成的6,855对ground-truth/重建图像上，使用语义探针、caption harshness、盲点率及受控退化进行分析。在此基础上提出BCI感知评估框架：四个VLM通过结构化提问对图像对进行评分，产生Tolerant Perceptual Alignment Score (T-PAS)和Tolerant Semantic Alignment Score (T-SAS)，再将VLM共识蒸馏为紧凑的BCI-Coherence Score (BCS)。

Result: 像素指标与语义一致性呈近零相关；表示学习指标混叠了感知与语义错误。提出的BCS在测试数据上实现T-PAS MAE=0.079 (r=0.700)、T-SAS MAE=0.082 (r=0.850)。人类验证中，联合一致性判断的Cohen's κ=0.882±0.174，Krippendorff's α=0.882。

Conclusion: BCI重建评估应从通用视觉相似性转向感知-语义可恢复性范式。BCS作为一个紧凑、可复用的评估器，在无需重复调用大规模VLM的前提下有效桥接了计算指标与人类判断之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: EEG-to-image evaluation should distinguish visual fidelity from recoverable meaning. Yet EEG-derived reconstructions are blurry, distorted, and low-detail, causing SSIM, LPIPS, and CLIP to penalize semantically recoverable outputs or reward plausible but incorrect ones. We analyze 6,855 ground-truth/reconstruction pairs from ATM, ENIGMA, BrainVis, and DreamDiffusion using semantic probes, caption harshness and blind-spot rates, and controlled degradations. Pixel metrics show near-zero correlation with semantic consistency, while representation metrics conflate perceptual and semantic errors. We therefore introduce a BCI-aware framework in which four VLMs assess image pairs through structured questions, producing Tolerant Perceptual Alignment Scores (T-PAS) and Tolerant Semantic Alignment Scores (T-SAS). Their consensus is distilled into the BCI-Coherence Score (BCS), a compact evaluator achieving a T-PAS MAE of 0.079 (r = 0.700) and a T-SAS MAE of 0.082 (r = 0.850) on our data. Human validation shows highly reliable joint coherence judgments, with Cohen's kappa = 0.882 +/- 0.174 and Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882, supporting perceptual-semantic recoverability over generic visual similarity. Code and resources are available at https://sukt03.github.io/BCS/.

</details>


### [37] [UMSS: Towards Unsupervised Multi-modal Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12372)
*Haitian Zhang,Thai Duy Nguyen,Xiangyuan Wang,Mohan Liu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出无监督多模态语义分割（UMSS）任务，并设计UniM2框架，通过跨模态对应协同（CMCS）学习统一潜在空间，结合跨模态协调器（CMH）以RGB为参考抑制模态间冲突，在NYU Depth v2和MFNet上分别提升mIoU 6.4%和9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语义分割依赖昂贵的人工标注，而无监督语义分割在单模态RGB上虽有效，但直接扩展到多模态数据时会因不同传感器捕获的异质结构模式难以调和而出现融合退化，无法有效利用互补信息。因此，探索完全无标签条件下的多模态语义分割具有重要价值。

Method: 提出UniM2框架，基于DINOv3构建。核心包括：(1) 跨模态对应协同（CMCS）机制，驱动学习统一潜在空间以提取内在共享语义线索，无需标签引导的自适应融合；(2) 跨模态协调器（CMH），将RGB设为稳定参考模态，抑制不一致的关系监督信号，引导模型利用互补结构特征来缓解模态间冲突。

Result: 在NYU Depth v2和MFNet两个数据集上的实验表明，UniM2相比现有框架在mIoU上分别提升了6.4%和9.8%，在无监督多模态语义分割任务上展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: UniM2首次有效解决了无监督多模态语义分割问题，通过统一潜在空间学习和RGB参考协调机制，成功实现了无标签条件下多模态互补信息的有效融合，性能大幅超越现有方法。

Abstract: Multimodal semantic segmentation (MSS) is essential for robust perception in complex environments, yet its potential remains largely untapped because of the prohibitive cost of human annotations. While unsupervised semantic segmentation (USS) has achieved strong results on a single RGB modality, its naive extension to multimodal data is often hindered by fusion degradation. This occurs because, without explicit supervision, existing frameworks struggle to reconcile the heterogeneous structural patterns captured by different sensors and therefore fail to effectively exploit their complementary information. In this paper, we make the first attempt to address the novel problem of Unsupervised Multimodal Semantic Segmentation (UMSS), aiming to effectively exploit complementary sensor information in a fully label free setting. To this end, we propose UniM2 (Unified Multimodal), a novel framework built on DINOv3 that transforms conventional fusion methods into consistent performance gains. Our key idea is to learn a unified latent space driven by Cross Modal Correspondence Synergy (CMCS) to extract intrinsic shared semantic cues, bypassing the need for label guided adaptive fusion. To mitigate inherent intermodal conflicts, we introduce a Cross Modal Harmonizer (CMH) that designates RGB as a stable reference, effectively suppressing inconsistent relational supervision while guiding the model to exploit complementary structural features. Extensive experimental results on NYU Depth v2 and MFNet show that UniM2 improves mIoU by 6.4% and 9.8%, respectively, demonstrating clear advantages over existing frameworks for UMSS.

Conclusion: 通过将显式感知观测以工具调用形式融入MLLM推理，IQA-T1有效弥补了现有方法对低层次视觉退化的不敏感性，在性能和可解释性方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Image Quality Assessment (IQA) in open-world environments remains challenging due to limited generalization and interpretability. Recent approaches based on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) introduce textual reasoning for quality prediction, yet their judgments rely heavily on semantically biased internal representations, making them insensitive to low-level perceptual degradations. We propose IQA-T1, a tool-based visual evidence reasoning framework that augments MLLM reasoning with explicit perceptual observations. During inference, the model autonomously invokes specialized analysis tools to generate structured visual evidence, such as noise residual maps, gradient statistics, and frequency spectra, which are progressively integrated into the reasoning process. To support this paradigm, we construct Q-Tool, a dataset containing 11k multimodal reasoning chains grounded in tool-generated evidence. Extensive experiments on seven IQA benchmarks show that IQA-T1 achieves the best overall performance across datasets while producing interpretable and evidence-grounded quality assessments. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/zibuyu-02/IQA-T1.

</details>

*Zhirui Zhang,Tianhang Nan,Yong Ding,Zhuolun Song,Dayu Hu,Xiaoyu Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了两条用于评估因果推断多实例学习（Causal Inference MIL）方法的假设，核心思想是通过引入独立分类通道来增加深度特征多样性，从而消除WSI级诊断与非诊断证据子图之间的虚假关联，并在乳腺癌和非小细胞肺癌数据集上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断MIL方法在数字病理WSI分析中缺乏统一的理论评估框架，难以系统理解其消除虚假关联的内在机制，需要从理论上阐明这类方法为何有效。

Method: 提出两条假设：(1) 因果推断MIL引入的独立分类通道能有效补全WSI分类；(2) 新通道与基线通道提取的特征差异越大，消除虚假关联的效果越强。基于此框架，在乳腺癌和非小细胞肺癌数据集上对多种因果推断MIL方法进行评估验证。

Result: 两条假设均得到验证：独立分类通道确实能有效完成WSI分类任务；特征差异的增大与虚假关联消除效果呈正相关。在乳腺癌和非小细胞肺癌数据集上的实验支持了上述假设。

Conclusion: 该假设为因果推断在WSI分析中的应用提供了新的理论视角，揭示了因果推断MIL的核心机制——通过叠加并行独立通道、增加深度特征多样性来消除虚假关联。

Abstract: Causal inference using front door intervention and multi-instance learning (MIL) has advanced the analysis of Whole Slide Images (WSI) in digital pathology. These methods adjust feature distributions of subtle evidence sub-images to correctly associate them with WSI-level diagnoses. We propose and prove 2 hypotheses for evaluating such methods: 1) Causal inference MIL introduces an independent classification channel that effectively completes WSI classification; 2) Greater difference between features extracted by the new and baseline channels increases effectiveness in eliminating false correlations. This hypothesis describes the core of causal inference MILs: overlaying parallel, independent channels to eliminate false associations between WSI-level diagnostic and non-diagnostic evidence sub-images by increasing deep feature diversity. Based on these hypotheses, we evaluated several causal inference MILs on breast cancer and non-small cell lung cancer datasets. This hypothesis provides a new theoretical perspective for applying causal inference to WSI analysis.

</details>


### [40] [SeamGen: Artist-Aligned UV Seam Generation via Graph Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12379)
*Hao Xu,Yuqing Zhang,Yiqian Wu,Xueqi Ma,Ding Liang,Yan-Pei Cao,Ying-Tian Liu,Xiaogang Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeamGen 是一个基于流匹配生成模型的 UV 接缝生成方法，通过 Mesh Transformer 骨干网络从大量艺术家制作的接缝布局中学习分布，并利用流模型的修复能力实现局部细化和约束引导生成，从而生成更符合艺术家偏好和生产需求的 UV 布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动 UV 接缝放置方法依赖手工设计的优化目标或预训练模型的语义代理，与工业生产管线中艺术家实际使用的接缝模式存在偏差，无法满足实用生产需求。

Method: 提出 SeamGen：①使用流匹配生成模型学习边级别接缝标签的分布；②设计 Mesh Transformer 骨干网络，将局部图注意力（网格边上）与全局自注意力（顶点间）交替融合，实现网格原生学习；③利用流模型训练无关的修复能力，实现局部接缝细化和约束引导的接缝生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，SeamGen 生成的 UV 布局相比基于 distortion 和语义代理的基线方法，更符合艺术家偏好，感知质量更优。

Conclusion: 通过从专业接缝布局数据中学习先验，SeamGen 能够生成与艺术家偏好高度一致的 UV 接缝，为自动化 UV 展开提供了一种更贴合工业生产需求的新范式。

Abstract: UV seam placement is a critical yet labor-intensive step in 3D content creation, requiring artists to balance chart shape, seam concealment, and alignment with semantic and geometric features. Existing automatic methods are primarily based on per-object optimization, relying on handcrafted objectives to avoid distortion or on proxies from pretrained models to inject semantic information. However, these strategies are not always well aligned with seams used in industrial production pipelines, often resulting in layouts that deviate from artist-preferred seam patterns and practical production requirements. To address these limitations, we propose SeamGen, a generative model for UV seam generation that aligns with artist preferences and production requirements. Instead of depending on manually designed objectives and constraints, SeamGen learns the distribution of per-edge seam labels from a large corpus of existing seam layouts using a flow-matching generative model. A key challenge is that typical Transformer architectures used in flow matching models are designed for sequential representations, such as point clouds, and cannot naturally account for mesh topology. To enable mesh-native learning, we design a Mesh Transformer backbone that interleaves local graph attention over mesh edges with global self-attention across vertices, capturing both fine-grained geometric cues and long-range topological coherence. To further improve inference-time controllability and quality, we exploit the training-free inpainting capability of flow models for both localized seam refinement and constraint-guided seam generation. Extensive experiments show that by learning priors from professional seam layout data, SeamGen produces UV layouts that better align with artist-authored preferences and achieve superior perceptual quality compared with distortion-based and semantic-proxy baselines.

</details>

*Yameng Zhang,Zhongyu Chen,Dianye Huang,Xiangyu Chu,K. W. Samuel Au,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合外部相机观测与B超图像的全局到局部位姿估计框架，通过跨模态融合与多尺度位姿损失抑制长扫描轨迹中的累积漂移，在体模与在体数据集上显著优于纯超声或纯相机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无 tracker 的 Freehand 3D 超声重建方法在长扫描轨迹中易受累积位姿误差影响，导致重建精度下降。纯超声方法的位姿估计漂移严重（>13 mm），亟需一种能结合全局稳定定位与局部组织运动线索的位姿估计方案。

Method: 提出 global-to-local 位姿估计框架：(1) 双相机分支：对多相机视角与时序观测进行上下文特征聚合，估计全局一致的探头轨迹；(2) B 超分支：对序列 US 图像进行解剖特征聚合，捕获组织依赖的局部运动线索；(3) 跨模态融合模块：整合相机上下文特征与解剖 US 特征，在位姿变换空间中预测残差，细化相机估计结果；(4) 多尺度位姿损失：约束多个时间尺度上的相对运动，抑制长时扫描中的累积漂移。

Result: 在两个自建数据集 FUSION-J 和 FUSION-L 上，US + Dual-Cam 模型将平均轨迹漂移降至 1.67 mm 和 1.29 mm，较双相机基线分别提升 16.50% 和 27.12%，远超纯 US 方法（>13 mm）。在在体前臂动脉重建中，Hausdorff 距离为 1.58 mm。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效结合了外部相机全局定位稳定性与 B 超局部解剖信息，显著抑制了长扫描轨迹中的累积漂移，在体模与临床场景中均展现出优越的 3D 重建精度，验证了其实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Freehand 3-D ultrasound (US) imaging has attracted increasing attention owing to its intuitive volumetric visualization, ease of use, and low cost. However, accurate 3-D reconstruction critically depends on stable probe pose estimation, yet existing trackerless methods remain susceptible to accumulated pose errors, particularly over long scanning trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose a global-to-local pose estimation framework that exploits external camera observations for globally stable localization and B-mode US images for anatomy-aware local refinement. Specifically, the framework comprises a dual-camera branch that performs contextual feature aggregation across camera views and temporal observations to estimate a globally consistent probe trajectory, and a B-mode branch that performs anatomical feature aggregation from sequential US images to capture tissue-dependent local motion cues. A cross-modal fusion module subsequently integrates the contextual camera features and anatomical US features to predict pose residuals and refine the camera-derived estimates in the transformation space. Furthermore, a multi-scale pose loss constrains relative motion over multiple temporal horizons to suppress accumulated drift during extended scans. The proposed framework is validated on phantom and in vivo datasets. On two in-house datasets (FUSION-J and FUSION-L) collected using different machines, the proposed US + Dual-Cam model reduces average trajectory drift to 1.67 mm and 1.29 mm, representing improvement of 16.50% and 27.12%, respectively, over a strong dual-camera baseline, while substantially outperforming US-only pose estimation (>13 mm drift). In in vivo forearm arteries reconstruction, it achieves Hausdorff distances of 1.58 mm, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method on real clinical scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [Physically Aware Radiomics Without Interpolation: Disentangling Voxel Geometry and Signal Modification in CT and MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12399)
*David Corral Fontecha,Juan Miranda Bautista,Pablo Menendez Fernández-Miranda,Sergio Rubio-Martín,Lara Lloret Iglesias,Jose A. Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种体素间距感知的影像组学框架，在不重采样的情况下将物理几何信息融入纹理计算，避免各向异性图像中各向同性重采样带来的信号失真。在CT肺结节和乳腺MRI上验证，该方法与原生提取高度一致，且保持了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统影像组学纹理特征在体素索引邻域内计算，隐式假设各向同性空间关系。但在各向异性图像中，各向同性重采样会将体素几何与插值引起的信号变化混为一谈，导致特征偏差。因此需要一种在保留原生图像信号的同时融入物理几何信息的提取方法。

Method: 基于PyRadiomics修改，实现体素间距感知（VS）的纹理特征提取。设置四种配置对比：原生非重采样提取（NR）、各向同性重采样（RS）、体素间距感知提取（VS）和伪各向同性预处理（FK，仅覆盖间距元数据不改图像数组）。在685例LIDC-IDRI肺结节CT和209例I-SPY2乳腺MRI上提取196个影像组学特征，采用ICC、受试者内变异、Friedman检验、特征选择、多层感知机及外部验证进行评估。

Result: VS与NR具有接近原生的一致性：CT中ICC(A,1)中位数为0.9976，MRI中为0.9984。RS一致性较低且偏差较大，FK表现居中，证实间距元数据单独即可影响组学特征。梯度和邻域敏感型描述符受预处理影响最大。CT外部验证中VS保持了与NR相当的预测性能，而MRI在不同预处理策略和分类器间变异较大。

Conclusion: 体素间距感知提取将几何建模与插值引起的信号修改解耦，保留原生图像信号，为各向异性CT和MRI的影像组学分析提供了优于各向同性重采样的一致性替代方案。

Abstract: Objective: Radiomic texture features are usually computed in voxel-index neighborhoods, implicitly assuming isotropic spatial relationships. In anisotropic images, this can confound voxel geometry with interpolation-induced signal changes. We developed a voxel-spacing-aware radiomic framework that incorporates physical geometry into texture computation without resampling.
  Approach: We modified PyRadiomics to account for voxel spacing while preserving the native image signal. Four configurations were compared: native non-resampled extraction (NR), isotropic resampling (RS), voxel-spacing-aware extraction (VS), and fake-isotropic preprocessing (FK), in which spacing metadata were overwritten without altering the image array. Experiments included 685 LIDC-IDRI pulmonary nodules and 209 I-SPY2 breast MRI cases, with 196 radiomic descriptors. Robustness was assessed using ICC, within-subject variability, Friedman testing, feature selection, machine learning, a multilayer perceptron, and external validation.
  Main results: VS showed near-native agreement with NR: median ICC(A,1) was 0.9976 in CT and 0.9984 in MRI. RS produced lower agreement and larger deviations, while FK showed intermediate behavior, confirming that spacing metadata alone can affect radiomic features. Gradient-derived and neighborhood-sensitive descriptors were most affected by preprocessing. VS preserved predictive performance comparable to NR in external CT validation, whereas MRI showed greater variability across preprocessing strategies and classifiers.
  Significance: Voxel-spacing-aware extraction separates geometric modeling from interpolation-induced signal modification while preserving the native image signal, offering a coherent alternative to isotropic resampling for radiomic analysis of anisotropic CT and MRI.

</details>


### [43] [Contrastive-Augmented Flow Matching for Style-Content Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12404)
*Yusong Li,Pingchuan Ma,Ming Gui,Vincent Tao Hu,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAtFM 将对比正则化融入可逆流匹配框架，在预测终点施加对比监督以促进内容-风格解耦，在多个基准上提升了检索、聚类分离和开集鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型以生成目标为主训练时，常产生纠缠或错位的表征因子，缺乏对内容与风格的结构化解耦。因此需要一种方法在保持生成能力的同时，显式促进解耦表征的学习。

Method: 提出 Contrastive Augmented Flow Matching (CAtFM)，在流匹配的可逆传输框架中引入对比正则化。关键设计是不约束中间隐变量或速度场，而是在训练的预测终点上施加对比监督，强制跨传输分布的语义一致性，使解耦隐式涌现，且不要求严格纯化或完全因子化的内容/风格表征。实验主要在 CLIP 嵌入空间进行，并使用冻结的 DINO 和 ALIGN 编码器验证。

Result: 在合成数据、域内风格及多个真实基准（ImageNet、WikiArt、DomainNet、DTD）上，CAtFM 在内容与风格检索、嵌入簇分离度及开集鲁棒性方面均优于生成式和判别式基线方法。

Conclusion: CAtFM 提供了一种将判别性约束与确定性传输简单耦合的方案，有效改善了分布偏移下的解耦能力和鲁棒性，为可控生成与组合泛化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Learning representations that separate content and style is crucial for controllable generation and compositional generalization. However, diffusion and flow-based models trained primarily with generative objectives often produce entangled or misaligned factors. To address this gap, we introduce Contrastive Augmented Flow Matching (CAtFM), a framework that integrates contrastive regularization into an invertible flow matching formulation to promote structured content-style representations. Rather than constraining intermediate latents or velocity fields, we apply contrastive supervision to predicted endpoints during training, enforcing semantic consistency across transported distributions while allowing disentanglement to emerge implicitly, without assuming strictly pure or fully factorized content and style representations. Our main experiments operate in the CLIP embedding space, with additional validation using frozen DINO and ALIGN encoders. Across synthetic data, in-domain styles, and real-world benchmarks (ImageNet, WikiArt, DomainNet, and DTD), CAtFM improves content and style retrieval, enhances embedding cluster separation, and achieves stronger open-set robustness compared to generative and discriminative baselines. Overall, CAtFM provides a simple way to couple discriminative constraints with deterministic transport, improving disentanglement and robustness under distribution shift.

</details>


### [44] [Virtual Chromoendscopy with Tunable Visibility Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12416)
*Yuhi Kanno,Yusuke Monno,Sho Suzuki,Tomohiro Tada,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟增强染色内镜（V-ECE），将虚拟染色内镜与图像增强技术结合，并通过一个用户可调增强水平的统一图像翻译模型，灵活生成不同增强程度的V-ECE图像，以改善胃部病变的可视化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统染色内镜（CE）需喷洒靛蓝胭脂红染料，虽能提升胃部病变（如早期癌）的可视性，但准备和喷洒染料增加了额外成本和时间。现有虚拟染色内镜（V-CE）通过图像翻译模型从标准内镜图像生成CE图像，但未能进一步优化病变可见性，且不同病变和医生偏好需要不同的增强水平，现有方法缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出虚拟增强染色内镜（V-ECE），将V-CE与图像增强技术融合；设计一种新颖的图像翻译模型，通过引入用户可调的增强水平参数，使单一统一模型能够生成不同增强程度的V-ECE图像。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提统一模型能够逼真地生成具有多种增强水平的V-ECE图像，验证了方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将虚拟染色内镜与可调图像增强相结合，为内镜医师提供了灵活、经济且高效的胃部病变可视化工具，有望减少传统CE的额外成本和时间，同时满足个性化的临床需求。

Abstract: Chromoendoscopy (CE) is a common clinical practice that sprays indigo carmine blue dye onto the gastric surface to improve the visibility of gastric lesions, such as an early cancer. While CE is effective in detecting the lesions, preparing and spraying the dye needs additional cost and time, which is undesirable both for patients and medical practitioners. To overcome this issue, virtual chromoendoscopy (V-CE) was recently proposed, which applies a learned image translation model to virtually generate a CE image from a standard endoscopy (SE) image. In this paper, we propose virtual enhanced chromoendoscopy (V-ECE) that combines V-CE with image enhancement techniques to further improve the visibility of gastric lesions. Because a desired enhancement level depends on the inspected lesion and the practitioner's preference, we introduce a novel image translation model that can generate V-ECE images using an enhancement level tunable by a user. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed model can plausibly generate V-ECE images with various enhancement levels using a unified model.

</details>


### [45] [MQAdapter: Multi-Modal Quantum Adapter for Coarse-to-Fine VLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12418)
*Yumiao Zhao,Bo Jiang,Min Lu,Xiao Wang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MQAdapter，一种面向小样本学习的多模态量子适配器，采用从粗到细的策略，利用量子计算中的纠缠与叠加特性在高维希尔伯特空间中建模高阶跨模态交互，从而增强视觉语言模型对视觉相似类别的细粒度判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM适配器主要关注视觉与文本表示的全局对齐，但未能利用语义相似类别来细化细粒度视觉表征，导致VLMs在Top-K上表现良好但Top-1精度不理想。

Method: 首先检索与输入图像最相似的Top-K候选类别作为语义锚点；然后将视觉和文本特征编码为量子态，在高维希尔伯特空间中利用量子纠缠和叠加机制建模高阶跨模态交互，在锚点引导下细化视觉特征。

Result: 在15个数据集上的评估表明，MQAdapter在使用更少可训练参数的情况下取得了有效性，并可集成到现有微调算法中进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: MQAdapter通过量子计算增强的跨模态学习，以参数高效的方式解决了VLM小样本分类中的细粒度判别难题，展现出良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language Models have demonstrated impressive transfer learning capabilities across a wide range of tasks. For few-shot classification, we observe that VLMs exhibit a notable ability to filter candidate categories and thus achieve high Top-K accuracy. However, they often struggle with fine-grained discrimination among visually similar categories, resulting in unsatisfactory Top-1 performance, as shown in Figure 1. Existing studies on VLM adapters generally focus on global alignment between visual and textual representations in the feature space, but fail to exploit semantically similar categories to refine fine-grained visual representations. Based on these observations, we propose a novel coarse-to-fine VLM fine-tuning approach for few-shot learning that leverages quantum computation, termed the Multi-Modal Quantum Adapter (MQAdapter). Specifically, MQAdapter first retrieves the Top-K category candidates most similar to the input image and uses them as semantic anchors. It then employs a cross-modal quantum learning mechanism to refine visual features under the guidance of these anchors. The core of this mechanism is the encoding of visual and textual features into quantum states. By leveraging quantum entanglement and superposition in a high-dimensional Hilbert space, MQAdapter effectively models higher-order cross-modal interactions, producing more discriminative representations than traditional Euclidean adapters. MQAdapter is parameter-efficient and can be integrated with various existing fine-tuning algorithms to achieve further performance gains. Evaluations on 15 datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of MQAdapter while requiring fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [46] [DeGuNet: Depth-Guided Ultra-Compact Backbones for Efficient LiDAR-Camera 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12419)
*Haifa Zhang,Yijing Wang,Peixi Peng,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeGuNet 提出一种专为深度引导表示学习设计的超紧凑、即插即用图像骨干网络，通过稀疏感知特征提取对齐多视图图像与非结构化LiDAR深度，大幅降低多模态3D检测的参数量与计算开销，并在nuScenes数据集上实现最高6.20 mAP的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态3D检测框架严重依赖大规模2D语义任务预训练的视觉骨干网络，导致大量参数冗余和结构错位——2D先验难以处理用于鸟瞰图几何的LiDAR投影的极端稀疏性。

Method: 提出DeGuNet，一个专为深度引导表示学习设计的超紧凑即插即用图像骨干网络，核心是引入稀疏感知特征提取机制，使多视图图像与非结构化LiDAR深度对齐，同时严格防止无效区域污染。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，集成到现有基线后：GPU内存消耗降低最高66.5%，推理速度提升1.16倍，同时实现最高6.20绝对mAP增益。

Conclusion: DeGuNet从根本上消除了多模态框架中的架构冗余，为参数高效的多模态3D感知建立了新范式。

Abstract: In autonomous driving perception, the fusion of LiDAR and camera modalities has become the dominant paradigm for 3D object detection. However, current multi-modal frameworks heavily rely on massive visual backbones pretrained on 2D semantic tasks. This reliance introduces substantial parameter redundancy and a structural misalignment, as 2D priors are ill-equipped to handle the extreme sparsity of LiDAR projections required for Bird's-Eye-View geometry. To address this, we present DeGuNet, an ultra-compact and plug-and-play image backbone explicitly designed for depth-guided representation learning. By incorporating sparsity-aware feature extraction mechanisms, DeGuNet effectively aligns multi-view images with unstructured LiDAR depth while strictly preventing invalid-region contamination. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate DeGuNet's broad plug-and-play applicability and superior efficiency. When integrated into established baselines, it fundamentally eliminates architectural redundancy, reducing GPU memory consumption by up to 66.5% and achieving a 1.16x inference speedup. Concurrently, DeGuNet delivers up to a 6.20 absolute mAP gain, establishing a new paradigm for parameter-efficient multi-modal 3D perception.

</details>


### [47] [More Than Where You Are: Learning Semantics, Structure, and Geometry from Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12429)
*Mao Chen,Xiangkai Zhang,Zhiyong Liu,Chuankai Liu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视跨视图定位问题，认为其不仅是2D匹配或位姿估计，更应成为模型在极端视角变化下建立一致性跨视图理解的途径。为此，作者提出CROSS框架，通过3D grounded对齐、结构感知匹配和假设排序三项设计，同时学习稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何，在KITTI和VIGOR数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视图定位方法存在三个核心局限：(1) 缺乏显式的3D grounding，模型难以建立三维空间理解；(2) 依赖严格的逐点匹配，可能削弱语义一致性；(3) 使用绝对目标学习，对几何推理提供有限的引导。这些问题阻碍模型在极端视角变化下形成稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何的一致性跨视图理解能力。

Method: 提出CROSS统一框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 3D-grounded alignment：将跨视图对齐建立在3D几何基础上，使结构学习成为内在需求；(2) structure-aware matching：结构感知匹配，鼓励语义表征保持稳定；(3) hypothesis ranking：假设排序机制，替代绝对目标，使模型获得可迁移的几何推理能力。

Result: 在KITTI和VIGOR数据集上，CROSS在跨视图定位任务中达到state-of-the-art性能。更重要的是，模型有效学习到了极端不同视角下的稳定语义、可靠结构和可迁移几何表征。

Conclusion: 跨视图定位不应仅被视为2D匹配或位姿估计问题。通过3D grounding、结构感知匹配和假设排序，CROSS框架成功使模型在极端视角变化下获得一致性的跨视图理解能力，为空间智能的发展提供了新路径。

Abstract: Consistent cross-view understanding under extreme viewpoint changes is essential for spatial intelligence, as it enables models to recognize the same scene across extreme viewpoint gaps. Cross-view localization naturally provides a promising pathway toward this ability, as it requires a model to align ground-view imagery with geo-referenced satellite-view imagery despite drastic appearance changes to estimate camera poses. Recent visual foundation models have made this long-standing localization problem increasingly feasible by providing rich 2D representations for cross-view matching. However, we argue that cross-view localization should not be viewed merely as 2D matching or pose estimation. In this work, we revisit cross-view localization as more than pose estimation and investigate how it can help the model develop consistent cross-view understanding under extreme viewpoint changes, including stable semantics, reliable structure, and transferable geometry. We identify three key limitations of existing methods that prevent them from achieving this. They usually lack explicit 3D grounding, rely on strict point-wise matching that can weaken semantic consistency, and learn from an absolute objective that provides limited guidance for geometric reasoning. To address these limitations, we propose CROSS, a unified cross-view localization framework built upon 3D-grounded alignment, structure-aware matching, and hypothesis ranking. This formulation makes structure learning an intrinsic requirement, encourages semantic representations to remain stable, and enables the model to acquire transferable geometry. Extensive experiments on the KITTI and VIGOR datasets show that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-view localization. More importantly, CROSS effectively learns stable semantics, reliable structure, and transferable geometry across extremely different viewpoints.

</details>


### [48] [ARDepth: Auto-regressive Monocular Depth Estimation with Progressive Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12433)
*Zijie Wang,Wei Zhang,Weiming Zhang,Xiao Tan,Weikai Chen,Xiaoxu Li,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARDepth 将单目深度估计形式化为结构化自回归生成，通过尺度递进条件注入和语义感知引导，逐步在不同空间分辨率下构建深度表示，在保持全局几何一致性的同时捕获细粒度局部细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型将深度恢复视为全局平滑场的迭代去噪，未能显式反映场景几何的分段性和尺度依赖性——几何结构是跨空间尺度层次化涌现的（粗布局→表面→边界）。

Method: 提出 ARDepth：1）将深度估计转化为随空间分辨率提升逐步构建的自回归生成过程；2）Scale-Progressive Conditioning (SPC)：在每个生成阶段注入多尺度视觉特征；3）Semantic-Aware Guidance (SAG)：提供场景级语义先验以增强全局结构一致性。

Result: 实验表明 ARDepth 取得强性能，生成的深度预测在不同尺度上结构一致，验证了自回归生成作为几何建模替代范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 自回归生成是扩散模型之外一个有前景的深度估计范式，其层次化、尺度递进的生成方式更贴合场景几何的本质组织结构。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently become the dominant paradigm for monocular depth estimation (MDE). However, they implicitly assume that depth can be recovered as a globally smooth field through iterative denoising, which does not explicitly reflect the piecewise and scale-dependent organization of scene geometry. In practice, geometric structure emerges progressively across spatial scales, where coarse layout, surfaces, and boundaries are constructed in a hierarchical manner. Motivated by this observation, we introduce ARDepth, which formulates depth estimation as structured auto-regressive generation. Instead of recovering depth through global refinement, ARDepth progressively constructs depth representations as spatial resolution increases. To support this generative process, we introduce Scale-Progressive Conditioning (SPC) to inject multi-scale visual features at each generation stage, and Semantic-Aware Guidance (SAG) to provide scene-level semantic priors that enhance global structural consistency. Together, these designs enable the model to capture fine-grained local details while maintaining coherent global geometry. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance and produces structurally consistent depth predictions across scales, validating auto-regressive generation as a promising alternative paradigm for geometric modeling.

</details>


### [49] [Let RGB Be the Language of Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12450)
*Timing Yang,Jinrui Yang,Xinlong Li,Yuhan Wang,Haoran Li,Yanqing Liu,Guoyizhe Wei,Jixuan Ying,Chen Wei,Rama Chellappa,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RINO（RGB In and RGB Out）范式，将各类视觉信息（掩码、深度图等）统一表示为RGB图像，将所有视觉任务转化为RGB到RGB的图像编辑问题，使单一模型无需任务特定微调即可在多种任务上实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉模型通常为不同任务设计独立的编码/解码架构和输出格式，缺乏统一的表示框架。作者受语言模型统一处理文本的启发，希望构建一个视觉领域的统一接口，使得多样化的视觉任务能够通过共享的"视觉语言"来表达和求解。

Method: RINO框架将一切输入（自然图像、掩码、深度图、姿态等结构化视觉信号）统一表示为RGB图像格式；将所有视觉任务统一建模为RGB到RGB的图像编辑问题。不同模态的视觉信息在内部共享相同的编解码架构和参数，基于通用的图像编辑主干网络，无需任何任务特定微调。

Result: RINO在密集理解任务（如语义分割、深度估计，输出统一为RGB格式）和密集条件生成任务（如姿态到图像生成，输入统一为RGB格式）上均展现出鲁棒且具有竞争力的零样本性能。

Conclusion: RINO范式证明了将多样化的视觉任务统一为RGB-to-RGB图像编辑问题的可行性，为构建通用统一的视觉-语言系统提供了有益洞见，推动以共享视觉语言表达、解释和求解各类视觉任务的研究方向。

Abstract: This work introduces a unified formulation for vision models, where diverse forms of visual information beyond natural images, such as masks, depth maps, and other structured visual signals, are all represented as RGB images, while general visual tasks can be converted into a common RGB-to-RGB image editing problem. In this paradigm, different types of visual information internally share the same encoding and decoding architecture and parameters as natural images, enabling a single model to transfer across tasks through a unified visual interface, in a way analogous to how language models operate over text. We refer to this formulation as RGB In and RGB Out (RINO). Built upon a generic image editing backbone without task-specific fine-tuning, RINO demonstrates robust and competitive zero-shot performance on both dense understanding tasks such as segmentation and depth estimation (where we unify outputs as RGB), and dense-conditioned generation tasks such as pose-to-image generation (where we unify inputs as RGB). We hope this study provides useful insights toward general unified vision-language systems, where diverse visual tasks can be expressed, interpreted, and solved through a shared visual language. Code is available at https://github.com/yangtiming/RINO.

</details>


### [50] [Steering Diffusion Models via Class-Contrastive Influence for Few-Shot Medical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12464)
*Jeeyung Kim,Erfan Esmaeili,Qiang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为类别对比影响（C2I）的新准则，用于量化和优化合成样本在少样本医学图像分类中的任务有用性。作者发现，高C2I样本是靠近决策边界的困难样本，能有效优化分类边界；基于此，采用强化学习以C2I为奖励微调扩散模型，引导生成更具类别信息量的样本，在多个少样本医学影像基准上显著提升了分类精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前利用扩散模型为少样本医学图像分类扩充训练集时，普遍关注提升合成图像的真实性、多样性或领域适应性，但忽略了更根本的问题：如何度量并优化样本对分类任务的实际有用性。生成样本并非同等有用，亟需一个面向下游任务效用的评估与优化准则。

Method: 提出Class-Contrastive Influence（C2I）指标，通过样本对分类器的梯度影响来量化其有用性：有效样本的损失梯度与同类的验证梯度对齐、与异类梯度对抗（即呈现较大的C2I差距）。分析表明，高C2I样本是靠近决策边界的困难样本。在此基础上，以C2I作为奖励信号，利用强化学习微调扩散模型，引导生成过程偏向高类别信息量的样本。

Result: 在多个少样本医学影像基准上，C2I引导的生成方法在分类精度和鲁棒性上均优于基于传统扩散模型增强的基线方法。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强的有效性应取决于任务有用性而非单纯的图像质量。C2I准则和相应的扩散模型微调策略为少样本医学图像分类中的生成式增强提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: When labeled data are scarce, off-the-shelf diffusion models can augment training sets for few-shot medical image classification, but not all generated samples are equally useful for the downstream task. Existing approaches largely improve synthetic data by increasing realism, diversity, or domain adaptation, while overlooking a more fundamental question: how should sample usefulness for classification be measured and optimized? We address this with Class-Contrastive Influence (C2I), a criterion that quantifies a sample's usefulness through its gradient-based influence on the classifier. We find that effective samples exhibit a strong C2I gap: their loss gradients align with validation gradients from the same class and oppose those from other classes. Our analysis further suggests that such high-C2I samples are hard, boundary-proximal examples that help refine the decision boundary and improve robustness. Building on this insight, we fine-tune diffusion models with reinforcement learning using a C2I-based reward to steer generation toward class-informative samples. Across several few-shot medical imaging benchmarks, C2I-guided generation improves downstream accuracy and robustness over diffusion-based augmentation baselines, showing that synthetic augmentation is most effective when guided by task usefulness rather than image quality alone.

</details>


### [51] [Self in Space: Benchmarking Self-Awareness and Spatial Cognition in UAV Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12477)
*Zhishan Zou,Guoyan Sun,Zhiwei Wei,Jiancheng Pan,Yujie Li,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIS-Bench是一个评估无人机具身空间智能的基准，通过"空间-自我"双维度和"感知-记忆-推理"三层级框架组织评估。研究发现当前多模态大语言模型在自我意识和动态建模方面存在显著缺陷，并提出融入光流和视觉特征融合的运动感知表示方法，可有效提升感知与记忆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机MLLM方法和基准主要关注以环境为中心的空间理解任务，忽视了智能体自身的自我意识（self-awareness）。具身场景不仅需要理解周围空间，还需要维护对智能体自身的连贯表征。为此，需要构建一个同时评估空间认知和自我意识的统一基准。

Method: 提出SIS-Bench基准，采用统一的"self-in-space"框架，沿空间（space）和自我（self）两个互补维度，以及感知（perception）、记忆（memory）、推理（reasoning）三个层级组织评估。数据集包含来自1646段真实无人机视频的4856个问答对，覆盖13个任务，通过任务条件化构建流程并经专家验证。此外，提出一种运动感知表示方法，通过光流和视觉特征融合来融入与自我相关的动态信息。

Result: 当前MLLMs在建模动态和以智能体为中心的过程中存在根本性局限：空间认知和自我意识之间呈现明显不平衡，且性能随认知层级递进而逐步退化。引入运动感知表示后，感知和记忆性能在空间认知和自我意识两方面均有持续改善，并能泛化到下游无人机决策任务。

Conclusion: 自我意识对于推进具身空间智能至关重要。SIS-Bench为"self-in-space"建模提供了新的基准和实证证据，运动感知的自我表征是提升无人机具身智能的有效方向。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV systems increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to operate in complex real-world environments. Such embodied scenarios require not only understanding the surrounding space but also maintaining a coherent representation of the agent itself. However, existing UAV-oriented approaches and benchmarks remain largely environment-centric, primarily focusing on spatial understanding tasks, with the agent's self-awareness remaining implicit. To address this gap, we introduce SIS-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence in UAV scenarios under a unified self-in-space formulation. SIS-Bench organizes evaluation along two complementary dimensions, space and self, and a three-level hierarchy of perception, memory, and reasoning. It contains 4,856 question--answer pairs across 13 tasks derived from 1,646 real-world UAV videos through a task-conditioned construction pipeline with expert verification.Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs exhibit fundamental limitations in modeling dynamic and agent-centered processes. In particular, we observe a clear imbalance between spatial cognition and self-awareness, as well as a progressive performance degradation across cognitive levels.Motivated by these findings, we further explore a motion-aware representation that incorporates self-related dynamics through optical flow and visual feature fusion. Experimental results show that modeling agent motion consistently improves perception and memory performance, not only in spatial cognition but also in self-awareness, and generalizes to downstream UAV decision-making tasks.Our results highlight the importance of self-awareness for advancing embodied spatial intelligence, and provide both a new benchmark and empirical evidence for motion-aware self-in-space modeling.

</details>


### [52] [RealSkin: Spatio-Spectral Partial Neural Adjoint Maps for Image-to-3D Attribute Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12495)
*Jing Li,Yawei Luo,Xiangze Meng,Ying Li,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealSkin 提出一种自监督框架，在学习的谱域中进行对应关系优化，通过空间引导的配准算法和谱感知神经伴随网络，解决了真实图像到合成3D模型的外观迁移中拓扑不一致和非等距匹配的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将真实图像的视觉属性迁移到合成3D表面时，传统方法面临分辨率不匹配和点对应离散性的问题；基于函数映射的方法虽能平滑传播属性，但依赖近等距假设和拓扑一致性，难以处理真实扫描与合成模型之间的拓扑差异和部分对应。

Method: 提出RealSkin自监督框架：1）空间引导配准算法，在严重拓扑差异下建立粗对应；2）谱感知神经伴随网络，将部分对应融入神经函数空间，建模非等距残差以细化对应关系，从而在学习的谱域中完成对应优化。

Result: 在具有挑战性的真实到合成场景上达到 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: RealSkin 通过谱域学习和神经伴随网络有效放宽了传统函数映射的等距假设和拓扑一致性约束，为真实到合成的外观迁移提供了鲁棒解决方案，代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D assets requires bridging the appearance gap between real-world observations and synthetic models. A promising approach is to transfer visual attributes from real images onto synthetic 3D surfaces. Traditional methods struggle with resolution mismatch and the inherent discreteness of point correspondences. In contrast, resolution-robust functional maps enable smooth attribute propagation but rely on near-isometry assumptions and topological consistency. To address these limitations, we propose RealSkin, a self-supervised framework that performs correspondence optimization in a learned spectral domain, guided by spatial correspondences. We first introduce a spatial-guided registration algorithm to establish coarse correspondences under severe topological discrepancies. To relax strict isometric assumptions and handle partial correspondences, we further design a spectral-aware neural adjoint network that incorporates partial correspondences into a neural function space and models non-isometric residuals for correspondence refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging real-to-synthetic scenarios. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [53] [TerraLogic: A Benchmark for Hierarchical Geospatial Reasoning in Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12497)
*Yuhang Yan,Linchao Mou,Bokang Yang,Qingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TerraLogic是一个面向遥感领域认知级地理空间推理的基准数据集，包含545个分层场景驱动任务，并提出工具增强型智能体HieraPlan，通过功能层次化和容错推理实现稳健的长期规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感研究多聚焦于感知任务，大型语言模型驱动的工具增强智能体在认知级地理空间推理方面尚未被充分探索，缺乏相应基准和有效方法。

Method: 构建TerraLogic基准（545个分层场景任务，涵盖光学、SAR、红外影像），提出HieraPlan智能体——将工具包组织为功能层次结构并具备容错推理能力，支持结构化抽象、工具故障恢复与长期规划。

Result: 实验表明现有方法在分层地理空间推理上表现欠佳，而HieraPlan在推理能力、跨模态泛化和错误处理方面均提供了强有力的基线。

Conclusion: TerraLogic填补了遥感领域认知推理评估的空白，HieraPlan为该方向提供了有效的基础方法，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Beyond perception, reasoning is essential in remote sensing for advanced interpretation, inference, and decision-making. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled tool-augmented agents that leverage external tools to perform complex analytical tasks. However, existing studies in remote sensing primarily focus on perception-oriented tasks, leaving cognitive geospatial reasoning largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce TerraLogic, a benchmark for geospatial reasoning. TerraLogic comprises 545 scenario-driven, hierarchy-aware tasks, such as hazard vulnerability assessment, urban heat island analysis, and forest fragmentation dynamics, spanning optical, Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR), and infrared (IR) imagery. It advances evaluation beyond recognition and monitoring toward cognitive-level geospatial analysis. To facilitate evaluation on TerraLogic, we further propose HieraPlan, a tool-augmented agent that organizes toolkits into functional hierarchies and performs fault-tolerant reasoning. HieraPlan enables structured abstraction, robust recovery from tool failures, and stable long-horizon planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that current approaches struggle with hierarchical geospatial reasoning, while HieraPlan provides a strong baseline with improved reasoning, cross-modal generalization, and error handling. The dataset and agent code are publicly available at https://github.com/Ireliya/TerraLogic.

</details>


### [54] [DynTrace: Tracking Dynamic Object Evidence for 4D Spatio-Temporal Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12503)
*Rongxin Gao,Yuzhi Huang,Dongxuan Liu,Chu Li,Zhenye Wang,Jie Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Xinghao Ding,Xiaotong Tu,Yue Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynTrace是一个无需训练的四维时空推理框架，通过动态轨迹可视化（DTV）和动态轨迹Token（DT-Token）两个互补组件，持续追踪动态物体证据，将几何信息融入视觉先验并构建结构化时空轨迹，显著提升了开源多模态大语言模型在连续动态场景感知上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在四维时空推理中存在关键局限：依赖稀疏帧级观测，导致连续动态线索碎片化，且无法区分解耦真实物体运动与相机引起的表观运动。受人类在追踪动态线索时能够补偿视角变化的启发，本文旨在解决这一不足。

Method: 提出DynTrace，一个无需训练的框架，包含两个互补组件：(1) 动态轨迹可视化（DTV），将世界坐标系下的轨迹重投影到图像平面，提供几何信息引导的视觉先验，以解耦真实物体动态与相机引起的表观运动；(2) 动态轨迹Token（DT-Token），组织为动态轨迹图（DTG），追踪物体级动态线索、轨迹演变和关键时刻，维护连续的动态物体证据用于连贯的四维推理。

Result: DynTrace持续提升多个开源多模态大语言模型的性能，在Dyn-Bench、VLM4D和DSI-Bench三个基准数据集上均取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 追踪动态物体证据对于鲁棒的四维时空推理至关重要，DynTrace通过几何信息引导的视觉先验和结构化时空轨迹实现了这一点，验证了该方法在连续动态场景感知中的有效性。

Abstract: 4D spatio-temporal reasoning, jointly modeling 3D spatial structure and temporal evolution, is essential for understanding dynamic worlds and enabling embodied interaction. While current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong capabilities in static scene understanding and coarse-grained 4D tasks, they still have notable limitations in continuous dynamic scene perception, especially in tracking dynamic object evidence for coherent 4D spatio-temporal reasoning. This shortcoming stems mainly from relying on sparse frame-level observations, fragmenting continuous dynamic cues and leaving models unable to disentangle genuine object dynamics from camera-induced apparent motion. Inspired by humans tracking dynamic cues while compensating for viewpoint changes, we propose DynTrace, a training-free framework for 4D spatio-temporal reasoning with two complementary components. Dynamic Trajectory Visualization (DTV) reprojects world-coordinate trajectories onto the image plane, providing geometry-informed visual priors that disentangle genuine object dynamics from camera-induced apparent motion. Meanwhile, the Dynamic Trace Token (DT-Token), organized into a Dynamic Trace Graph (DTG), tracks object-level dynamic cues, trace evolution, and key moments, maintaining continuous dynamic object evidence for coherent 4D reasoning. Together, these two components equip MLLMs with continuously tracked dynamic object evidence, grounded in geometry-informed visual priors and structured spatio-temporal traces. DynTrace consistently improves open-source MLLMs, achieving state-of-the-art results on Dyn-Bench, VLM4D, and DSI-Bench, validating the importance of tracking dynamic object evidence for robust 4D spatio-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [55] [DiTailed: Ensuring Visual Object Consistency in Text-Image-to-Image Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12539)
*Francesco Taioli,Daniel Coelho,Iaroslav Melekhov,Roberto Alcover-Couso,Jose Miguel Grande Saiz,Virginia Fernandez Arguedas,Artur Bekasov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本引导图像编辑中对象一致性问题，提出了ABO-Edit数据集和FlowMirror方法——利用矫正流模型条件嵌入空间中编码最终生成图像预测的特性，设计了一个无参数的辅助损失来监督该嵌入空间，从而提升编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本引导的图像编辑模型在保持视觉对象一致性（即编辑过程中保持主体关键属性不变）方面存在明显不足，亟需针对性的数据集和有效的改进方法。

Method: 1）构建ABO-Edit数据集：包含12,000+组三元组（源图像、编辑提示、目标图像），源自艺术家设计的3D资产，具备多视角覆盖和人工验证的质量控制；2）发现矫正流（rectified flow）模型的条件嵌入空间即使在高噪声水平下也编码了对最终生成图像的预测；3）提出FlowMirror：一个无参数的辅助损失函数，直接监督该条件嵌入空间，无需修改模型架构。

Result: FlowMirror在多个评估指标上相较于基线方法提升了生成质量，验证了条件嵌入空间监督的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘矫正流模型条件嵌入空间的潜在特性并施加辅助监督，FlowMirror以无参数、无架构修改的方式有效改善了文本引导图像编辑中的对象一致性问题。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in text-guided image editing, generative models frequently fail to preserve visual object consistency, defined as the preservation of a subject's key attributes throughout the editing process. We address this limitation through three contributions. First, we introduce ABO-Edit, a dataset specifically designed to study object consistency, comprising over 12,000 triplets of source images, editing prompts, and high-quality target images rendered from artist-designed 3D assets, with multi-view coverage and human-verified quality control. Second, we uncover an overlooked property of image-editing rectified flow models: the conditioning embedding space, not directly supervised during training, encodes a prediction of the final generated image even at high noise levels. Third, exploiting this finding, we propose FlowMirror, a parameter-free auxiliary loss that supervises this conditioning embedding space. Without architectural changes, our method improves generation quality across several metrics over baselines.

</details>


### [56] [Edge-Aware Thermal Infrared UAV Swarm Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12544)
*Yu-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向边缘计算的自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF）在线跟踪管道，在保持实时效率的同时，通过状态依赖的运动学建模增强线性KF，结合瞬时误检抑制和运动学驱动的预测滑行，提升热红外条件下微小无人机跟踪的轨迹连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机跟踪方法过度追求精度而忽视边缘部署的计算约束；标准KF效率高但其恒速假设在动态无人机运动和热传感器抖动下容易失效；非线性估计器虽鲁棒但计算开销大。需要一种兼顾精度与实时边缘推理的解决方案。

Method: 提出以自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF）为核心的在线跟踪管道。AKKF在标准线性KF基础上引入状态依赖的运动学建模（以保持实时性），同时集成了瞬时误检抑制（transient false-positive suppression）和运动学驱动的预测滑行（kinematics-driven predictive coasting）策略，以提升遮挡/检测丢失时的轨迹连续性。

Result: 在Beyond Strong Baseline (BSB)基准上联合评估跟踪性能与计算效率，为面向边缘的无人机跟踪提供了基准参考和洞察。

Conclusion: 所提出的边缘感知在线跟踪管道在热红外无人机跟踪任务中，能够在保持实时计算效率的前提下有效提升轨迹连续性与鲁棒性，为未来实时边缘部署提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Thermal infrared (TIR) imaging is essential for UAV swarm operations in visually degraded environments. However, tracking tiny UAVs remains challenging due to limited appearance cues, frequent occlusions, and rapid maneuvers. Despite significant progress driven by benchmarks such as the Anti-UAV challenge, existing methods primarily prioritize accuracy while overlooking the computational constraints of real-time edge deployment. The standard Kalman Filter (KF) offers the efficiency required for edge devices, yet its constant-velocity assumption often breaks down under highly dynamic UAV motion and thermal sensor jitter. More sophisticated nonlinear estimators can improve robustness but often introduce additional computational costs. To address this gap, we propose an edge-aware online tracking pipeline centered on the Adaptive Kinematic Kalman Filter (AKKF), which augments the linear KF with state-dependent kinematic modeling while preserving real-time efficiency. Combined with transient false-positive suppression and kinematics-driven predictive coasting, the presented pipeline improves trajectory continuity under challenging TIR conditions. Experiments on the Beyond Strong Baseline (BSB) benchmark provide a starting point for edge-aware UAV tracking by jointly evaluating tracking performance and computational efficiency, offering insights toward future real-time deployment.

</details>


### [57] [CGRL: Concept-Guided Pruning and Representation Learning for Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12556)
*Thuc Huynh,Tuan Le,Doanh C. Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CGRL 框架利用从疾病提示中提取的类别级概念原型，通过概念相关性剪枝筛选 top-K 相关图块，并通过概念引导的对比表示学习优化特征空间，在弱监督全切片图像分类任务上提升了准确率和宏观 F1，同时降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MIL 方法在弱监督 WSI 分类中主要依赖视觉线索聚合大量图块嵌入，容易保留大量非信息性图块，且实例特征与类别级疾病语义之间的对齐较弱。因此需要引入类别级语义概念作为先验，改善图块选择与表示学习。

Method: 提出 CGRL 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 概念相关性剪枝：通过计算图块实例与类别概念的相似度进行排序，保留 top-K 概念相关图块用于下游 MIL 聚合；(2) 概念引导的对比表示学习：基于同一相似度矩阵构建类别级别的正负图块集合，优化目标类别、对称辅助以及跨类别分离三个对比目标，正则化投影概念空间。

Result: 在 TCGA-BRCA 和 TCGA-NSCLC 数据集上，使用多种代表性 MIL 方法评估，CGRL 在多个模型-数据集组合上均有提升，尤其在准确率和宏观 F1 上改善明显，同时通过概念相关性剪枝降低了计算开销。提升幅度取决于下游 MIL 模型和具体数据集。

Conclusion: 类别级语义概念为弱监督计算病理学中的图块选择和表示学习提供了一种有效且实用的先验，CGRL 框架验证了该方法的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Weakly supervised whole-slide image (WSI) classification is widely used in computational pathology because slide-level labels are easier to obtain than dense region annotations. Existing multiple instance learning (MIL) methods often aggregate large bags of patch embeddings using mainly visual cues, which can retain many non-informative patches and provide weak alignment between instance features and class-level disease semantics. We propose Concept-Guided Pruning and Representation Learning (CGRL), a simple framework that introduces class-level concept prototypes derived from disease prompts into the MIL pipeline. First, concept-relevance pruning ranks patch instances by their similarity to class concepts and retains the top-K concept-relevant patches for downstream MIL aggregation. Second, concept-guided contrastive representation learning constructs class-wise positive and negative patch sets from the same similarity matrix and optimizes target-class, symmetric auxiliary, and cross-class separation objectives, thereby regularizing the projected concept space. We evaluate CGRL on TCGA-BRCA and TCGA-NSCLC using multiple representative MIL methods. Experimental results show that CGRL improves several model-dataset combinations, with gains depending on the downstream MIL model and dataset. It achieves particularly clear improvements in accuracy and macro-F1 while reducing computational cost through concept-relevance pruning. These findings demonstrate that class-level semantic concepts provide an effective and practical prior for patch selection and representation learning in weakly supervised computational pathology.

</details>


### [58] [Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Event-Aware Visual Allocation in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12557)
*Yifan Lu,Ziqi Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Jun Gao,Bing Li,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GMM-EVA 提出基于高斯混合模型的事件感知视觉分配策略，通过为每个事件保留一张高分辨率关键帧与多张低分辨率辅助帧，在仅用约一半视觉 token 预算的情况下，实现了与基线方法相当的长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 在长视频理解中面临两大困境：均匀采样导致计算成本过高和信息丢失；现有关键帧选择方法将视频帧视为原子实体并均等分配视觉预算，忽略了高层语义结构，引入了大量冗余。因此需要一种事件感知的差异化视觉分配策略。

Method: 提出 GMM-EVA，利用高斯混合模型（GMM）从离散的帧级观测中建模事件级结构；在此基础上采用差异化分配策略：每个事件保留一张高分辨率主关键帧以保持细节保真度，同时使用低分辨率辅助关键帧维持时序上下文并优化 token 预算。该方法无需训练、即插即用，可泛化至多种相关性度量及下游 LVLM。

Result: 在多个长视频基准上，GMM-EVA 显著优于均匀采样方法，在仅使用约一半视觉 token 预算的情况下，达到了与基线选择方法相当的性能，展现出优越的效率与有效性。

Conclusion: GMM-EVA 通过事件级建模与差异化分辨率分配，在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持了对长视频的高质量理解，是一种高效且通用的事件感知视觉分配框架。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) face significant challenges in long video understanding due to the excessive computational cost and information loss associated with uniform sampling. Existing keyframe selection methods often treat video frames as atomic entities and allocate visual budgets equally, thereby overlooking high-level semantic structures and introducing substantial redundancy. To address these limitations, we propose GMM-EVA (Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Event-Aware Visual Allocation), which leverages Gaussian Mixture Models to model event-level structure from discrete frame-wise observations. A differentiated allocation strategy is then applied to preserve one primary high-resolution keyframe per event for high-fidelity detail, while utilizing lower-resolution secondary keyframes to maintain temporal context and optimize token budgets. GMM-EVA is a training-free, plug-and-play framework that generalizes robustly across various relevance measures and downstream LVLMs. Extensive experiments on multiple long video benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms uniform sampling. Notably, GMM-EVA achieves comparable performance to baseline selection methods while utilizing only approximately half of the visual token budget, highlighting its superior efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [59] [Traceback Translators Against Forgetting in Continual Fake Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12569)
*Enrico Gottardis,Mattia Tamiazzo,Simone Milani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种抗遗忘的虚假语音检测方案：在冻结的检测器前端引入域翻译器（traceback translator network），将新数据的特征空间映射回原始特征空间，从而在无需重新训练检测器的情况下适应新的生成模型，既保持了对旧数据的高检测精度，又有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假语音检测器面临新型生成模型不断涌现的挑战，持续学习虽能更新模型以适应新数据，但会导致灾难性遗忘——即模型在先前见过的样本上性能显著下降。因此需要一种既能适应新数据、又不牺牲旧数据检测精度的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于域翻译器的抗遗忘方案：保持原有检测器参数冻结不变，在其前端部署一个"回溯翻译网络"（traceback translator network），该网络学习将新生成模型产生的特征空间映射回原始特征空间，使冻结检测器能够直接处理转换后的特征，从而识别新类型的虚假语音。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统重新训练方法相比，该策略在保持对先前数据检测精度的同时，实现了较高的新数据检测率，且大幅降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过域翻译器与冻结检测器相结合的方式，可以在虚假语音检测任务中有效缓解灾难性遗忘，兼顾对新旧数据的检测性能并节省计算资源，是一种实用的持续学习替代方案。

Abstract: Fake speech detectors are increasingly challenged by the development of new and more accurate generative models. To cope with this problem, continual learning techniques are nowadays widely considered feasible strategies for updating models to new datasets, but they also lead to decreased performance on previously seen samples (catastrophic forgetting). In this work, we propose a forgetting-resilient solution based on the adoption of domain translators within a frozen detector, which remaps the new feature spaces into the original ones by means of a traceback translator network. Experimental results show that this strategy enables the achievement of high detection rates with respect to traditional retraining, while minimizing the computational effort and preserving the detection accuracy on previous data.

</details>


### [60] [WanToFight: Real-Time Generative Game Engine for Multi-Player Combat Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12592)
*Li Hu,Guangyuan Wang,Peng Zhang,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WanToFight 是一个基于 Wan-1.3B 视频扩散 Transformer 的生成式游戏引擎，能够在单张 RTX 5090 上以 512×384 分辨率、30FPS 实时模拟《拳皇'97》双人对战，首次同时实现多人控制、实时推理、复杂物理交互和对抗性游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式游戏引擎要么仅针对单人第一人称场景，要么局限于非实时合作场景，多人控制、实时推理、复杂物理交互和对抗性游戏这四个需求从未被同时解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 系统基于 Wan-1.3B 视频扩散 Transformer，包含三个核心组件：(1) 流式自回归生成器，采用 block-causal attention 和滚动 KV 缓存；(2) 视觉锚定的玩家关联模块，将每个玩家的键盘信号绑定到对应角色身份；(3) 门控局部因果键盘注入模块，并通过单人-完整对战的课程学习策略训练。推理加速方面，采用四步 DMD 蒸馏的学生模型配合剪枝后的 VAE 解码器，实现实时性能。

Result: 在单张 NVIDIA RTX 5090 上以 512×384 分辨率、30FPS 持续运行完整对局，实现了实时双人对战的生成式游戏模拟。

Conclusion: WanToFight 是首个同时融合多人控制、实时推理、复杂物理交互和对抗性游戏的生成式游戏引擎，为生成式游戏引擎在多人实时对抗场景开辟了新可能。

Abstract: We present WanToFight, a generative game engine that simulates real-time, two-player The King of Fighters '97 (KOF~'97) gameplay from keyboard input. Prior generative game engines target either single-player first-person settings or non-real-time cooperative scenarios; multi-player control, real-time inference, complex physical interaction, and adversarial gameplay have not been jointly addressed. WanToFight closes this gap with three components built on the Wan-1.3B video diffusion transformer: a streaming autoregressive generator with block-causal attention and a rolling KV cache; a visually grounded Player Association module that binds each player's keyboard signal to a character identity; and a gated, locally causal keyboard injection module trained with a single-player-to-full-gameplay curriculum. A four-step DMD-distilled student paired with a pruned VAE decoder sustains 30FPS at 512x384 on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 over the duration of a complete match. To our knowledge, WanToFight is the first generative game engine to combine multi-player control, real-time inference, complex physical interaction, and adversarial gameplay in one system.

</details>


### [61] [Decouple and Reason: Anatomically Guided Two-Stage Voxel-Level Grounding of Free-Text Findings in 3D Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12602)
*Kwang-Hyun Uhm,Inhwa Son,Sung-Jea Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦框架DAGG，将3D胸部CT的自由文本视觉定位任务分解为类别无关的病灶分割和文本-体积推理两个阶段，并引入解剖学引导（相对空间坐标+肺叶先验）来解决空间歧义，在ReXGroundingCT基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法难以同时学习精确3D分割所需的局部特征表示和文本对齐所需的复杂语义理解，导致定位性能欠佳。需要一种能将检测与推理解耦的新范式来处理3D医学视觉定位的复杂性。

Method: 提出解耦框架DAGG，分为两阶段：(1) 类别无关的病灶分割——提取候选子体积，定位潜在异常区域；(2) 文本-体积推理——在候选子体积上进行跨模态对齐。推理模块中引入显式解剖学引导，利用相对空间坐标和肺叶先验信息来解决局部区域的空间歧义。

Result: 在ReXGroundingCT基准测试中，该方法在官方排行榜上的整体定位质量达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 将检测与推理解耦是处理3D医学视觉定位复杂性的高效范式，验证了解耦策略相较于端到端方法的优越性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Automatic voxel-level grounding of free-text findings in 3D chest Computed Tomography (CT) is critical for clinical interpretability. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intricate spatial complexity of large 3D volumes and the heterogeneity of free-text findings. Existing end-to-end approaches often struggle to simultaneously learn the localized feature representations required for accurate 3D segmentation and the complex semantic understanding needed for text alignment, leading to suboptimal grounding performance. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose a novel decoupled framework that disentangles the problem into two specialized stages: (1) class-agnostic lesion segmentation and (2) text-volume reasoning. This structural separation allows the model to first extract candidate sub-volumes by localizing potential abnormalities. Subsequently, intensive cross-modal reasoning is performed to align these localized sub-volumes with free-text medical findings. To resolve the spatial ambiguities inherent in local regions, the reasoning module is augmented with explicit anatomical guidance, utilizing relative spatial coordinates and lung lobe priors. Evaluated on the ReXGroundingCT benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in overall grounding quality on the official leaderboard. These results demonstrate that decoupling detection from reasoning is a highly effective paradigm for handling the complexity of 3D medical visual grounding. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/khuhm/DAGG.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Vision-Free CIR: Attribute-Augmented Scoring and LLM-Based Reranking for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12621)
*Ryotaro Shimada,Yu-Chieh Lin,Yuji Nozawa,Youyang Ng,Osamu Torii,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"无视觉"的组合图像检索框架，通过属性增强混合评分和LLM重排序两项技术，在CIRR数据集上取得了领先的零样本性能，揭示了语义推理与细粒度视觉匹配之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然"无视觉"方法在标准图像检索中有效，但在组合图像检索（CIR）这种需要同时理解参考图像和文本修饰的多模态任务中，纯文本描述存在固有的视觉信息丢失问题，其在CIR中的适用性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 提出两个核心技术：（1）**属性增强混合评分（Attribute-Augmented Hybrid Scoring）**：通过显式属性匹配来补偿文本描述中丢失的视觉细节；（2）**基于LLM的重排序（LLM-Based Reranking）**：对Top候选结果进行语义一致性验证，进一步提升检索精度。

Result: 在开放域CIRR数据集上，该方法达到44.04%的R@1，相比现有零样本CIR方法提升了8.79%。在FashionIQ上的结果揭示了语义推理与细粒度视觉匹配之间的权衡。消融实验证实了属性增强评分和LLM重排序两个模块均能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: "无视觉"范式在组合图像检索任务中是可行且有效的，属性增强评分和LLM重排序是关键使能技术，但该方法在需要细粒度视觉匹配的场景（如时尚检索）中仍存在局限性。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that "Vision-Free'' approaches (representing images as text) can be effective for standard image retrieval tasks. However, it remains unclear whether this paradigm can effectively handle a more complex, multimodal task, Composed Image Retrieval (CIR), due to the inherent information loss in textual descriptions. In this paper, we introduce a Vision-Free CIR framework that addresses this challenge through two key techniques: (1) Attribute-Augmented Hybrid Scoring, which compensates for lost visual details via explicit attribute matching, and (2) LLM-Based Reranking, which verifies semantic consistency of top candidates. Experiments on the open-domain CIRR dataset show that our approach outperforms existing Zero-shot CIR methods (44.04% R@1, +8.79%). On FashionIQ, our results highlight the trade-off between semantic reasoning and fine-grained visual matching. Ablation studies reveal that both attribute-augmented scoring and LLM-Based Reranking consistently improve performance.

</details>


### [63] [MAGE: Color-Invariant and Spatial Knowledge Distillation for Gastric Neoplasm Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12663)
*Jiho Jun,Jeongwon Woo,Jaemin Song,Thanh Bong Nguyen,Dong-heon Yeon,Donghoon Kang,Jae-Myung Park,Sung-Jea Ko,Kwang-Hyun Uhm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MAGE框架，通过在训练时引入仅依赖无色彩掩码图像的辅助分支，并以双目标蒸馏策略将结构特征迁移至主分支，从而在不需分割标注的推理条件下，实现胃癌与腺瘤的高精度分类和可解释的注意力图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃腺瘤与腺癌在内镜下鉴别极具挑战：两者类间相似性高、边界模糊；现有ROI方法存在检测/分割误差传播问题，全局分类方法缺乏空间聚焦；深度网络易被纹理偏差（出血、光照伪影等）误导，形成虚假相关而非学习真实形态特征。

Method: 提出MAGE框架：训练时引入辅助局部专家分支，该分支以掩码消色图像（屏蔽背景与颜色）为输入，强制学习纯结构判别特征；通过双目标蒸馏策略，将辅助分支的分类logits和空间注意力图共同迁移至主分支（全彩WLI输入），提供隐式空间监督，使主分支在保留临床相关色彩线索的同时，扎根于形态学特征进行预测。

Result: 在临床胃内镜数据集上，MAGE显著优于现有检测方法（如YOLO）和分类方法（如Swin-Transformer），在分类性能与可解释注意力图方面均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: MAGE通过掩码消色引导与双目标蒸馏，有效消除了纹理偏差和捷径学习，实现了基于形态学的高精度胃肿瘤分类，兼具实时可部署性和临床可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate differentiation between gastric adenoma and carcinoma during endoscopy is critical for clinical decision-making. Yet, this task is highly challenging due to high inter-class similarity and ambiguous boundaries between the two classes. Existing ROI-based classification methods often suffer from detection/segmentation error propagation and loss of surrounding global context. In contrast, full-image classification lacks the necessary spatial focus. Furthermore, we observe that deep neural networks gravitate towards domain-specific texture biases(e.g. bleeding, lighting artifacts), often causing models to predict based on spurious correlations instead of intrinsic morphological features. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework, Masked Achromatic Guidance Expert (MAGE). During training, we introduce an auxiliary local expert branch trained on masked achromatic views of the neoplasm. By suppressing background context and color, this branch is forced to learn highly discriminative, purely structural features. We then employ a dual-objective distillation strategy, transferring both classification logits and spatial attention maps to provide implicit spatial supervision to the main branch that receives full WLI as input. This dual-objective distillation forces the model to ground its predictions in morphology rather than relying on shortcuts, while still retaining clinically relevant color cues. At inference time, our deployable model operates on images without annotated masks, ensuring real-time deployability . Extensive experiments on a clinical gastric endoscopy dataset show that our method significantly outperforms existing detection-based methodologies (e.g. YOLO) and classification-based methodologies (e.g. Swin-Transformer), providing not only superior classification performance but also interpretable attention maps for clinical reliability.

</details>


### [64] [Text-Aided Multi-Modal Panoptic Symbol Spotting for CAD Floor Plan Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12678)
*Yan Gong,Bohao Li,Bowen Du,Junchen Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextCAD 是一个多模态框架，联合建模 CAD 平面图中的图形基元与文本标注，通过类型-属性关联编码器（TACE）和多级语义过滤（MSF）实现语义层次对齐，用于全景符号识别，达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CAD 全景符号识别方法以图形基元为中心，对文本标注利用不足；少数文本感知方法也仅停留于表面处理，未能正确建模标注的复杂语法和层次语义，导致语义丢失和次优识别效果。

Method: 提出 TextCAD 多模态框架：(1) 设计 Type-Attribute Correlation Encoder (TACE)，通过联合建模标注的类型与属性来显式编码标注的组合语义；(2) 构建 Semantic Hierarchy Alignment 框架，包含 Multi-level Semantic Filtering (MSF) 和基元下采样，在不同语义层级自适应对齐标注语义与图形基元，实现精确的跨模态语义注入与融合。

Result: 在真实建筑 CAD 数据集上的实验表明，TextCAD 有效提升了符号识别性能，并取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

Conclusion: 通过深度建模文本标注的组合语义和层次对齐，TextCAD 显著增强了 CAD 全景符号识别能力，验证了多模态语义融合在智能设计理解中的关键价值。

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design (CAD) floor plan drawings contain both graphical primitives and textual annotations, which provide complementary geometric and semantic cues for intelligent design understanding. Among CAD analysis tasks, panoptic symbol spotting has become increasingly important with the growing demand for industrial digitalization and deep learning-based automation. However, most existing methods remain primarily primitive-centric and underexploit textual annotations, despite their critical semantic value. Even the few text-aware approaches often treat annotations only superficially, without properly modeling complex syntax and hierarchical semantics of CAD annotations, which leads to semantic loss and suboptimal spotting performance. To address these limitations, we propose TextCAD, a multimodal framework that jointly models graphical primitives and textual annotations for panoptic symbol spotting. Specifically, we design a Type-Attribute Correlation Encoder (TACE) to explicitly encode the compositional semantics within annotations by jointly modeling their types and attributes. We further introduce a Semantic Hierarchy Alignment framework with Multi-level Semantic Filtering (MSF) and primitive downsampling, which adaptively aligns annotation semantics with graphical primitives at different semantic levels and enables accurate cross-modal semantic injection and fusion. Experiments on real-world building-design datasets show that TextCAD effectively improves symbol spotting performance and achieves state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [65] [ReflectVLN: Training Vision-Language Navigation Agents with Reflective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12680)
*Jiahang Wang,Yirong Yang,Yanqing Zhu,Minghua Luo,Shichao Xie,Fei Liu,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReflectVLN 提出了一种包含意图智能体与执行智能体双向交互的闭环VLN框架，通过子任务分解、进度监控和按需反思实现错误恢复，并引入Action-CoT训练策略以提升决策时序一致性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法缺乏显式的闭环机制来跟踪语义进度、诊断执行失败并从长距离导航中的误差累积中恢复，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出ReflectVLN框架：1）意图智能体负责子任务分解与反思，生成可执行子任务描述作为修正计划；2）执行智能体将子任务描述落实到短时域动作，同时监控子目标进度并检测偏离行为；3）两者之间通过双向闭环通信——执行智能体发射进度与偏离信号触发按需反思，意图智能体返回结构化指导以重新调节后续动作；4）引入Action Chain-of-Thought（Action-CoT），一种路径条件下的双查询训练方案，用于生成具有可解释中间推理的动作序列。

Result: 在标准VLN基准上，ReflectVLN在受限数据预算下提升了成功率和路径效率，训练成本较低，推理时高层意图调用次数更少，同时提供可解释的中间决策。

Conclusion: ReflectVLN通过双向交互的智能体架构和Action-CoT训练策略，有效实现了VLN中的闭环错误诊断与恢复，在效率、性能和可解释性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Existing vision-language navigation methods often couple a VLM with waypoint decoders to produce multi-step action plans, but they typically lack an explicit closed-loop mechanism for tracking semantic progress, diagnosing execution failures, and recovering from error accumulation in long-horizon navigation. To address this gap, we propose ReflectVLN, an agentic VLN framework that organizes decision-making through bidirectionally interactive intention and execution agents. The intention agent performs subtask decomposition and reflection, generating executable subtask descriptions as corrective plans. Conditioned on these descriptions, the execution agent grounds them into short-horizon actions under current observations while monitoring sub-goal progress and detecting off-track behavior. Crucially, ReflectVLN enables closed-loop bidirectional communication: the execution agent emits progress and deviation signals to trigger reflection and subtask updates on demand, and the intention agent returns structured guidance that reconditions subsequent actions for recovery. To encourage temporally coherent decisions with interpretable intermediate rationales, we introduce Action Chain-of-Thought (Action-CoT), a path-conditioned dual-query training scheme for action generation. Experiments on standard VLN benchmarks show that ReflectVLN improves success rates and path efficiency under a constrained data budget, with favorable training cost and fewer high-level intention calls at inference time, while providing interpretable intermediate decisions for analysis and collaboration. Code is available at: https://github.com/AIprogrammer/ReflectVLN

</details>


### [66] [MambaPSA: A Mamba-based Replacement for C2PSA in YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12681)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Chia-Min Lin,Hsin-Jui Pan,Ching-Yu Tsai,Chih-Hsiang Yang,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将Mamba状态空间模型集成到YOLO26目标检测框架中，用轻量级MambaPSA替换骨干网络末端的C2PSA模块，并在颈部P3/P4/P5层插入双向Vision Mamba模块。实验表明MambaPSA在几乎不损失精度的情况下显著降低参数量、FLOPs并提升CPU推理速度，P4层BiViM则带来最佳精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索状态空间模型（特别是Mamba）在NMS-free轻量级目标检测器中替代自注意力机制的可行性，寻求效率与精度之间更优的权衡。

Method: 提出MambaPSA模块替代YOLO26骨干网络末端的C2PSA注意力模块；同时将双向Vision Mamba（BiViM）模块分别插入颈部网络的P3、P4、P5层级进行对比实验。在PASCAL VOC 2007+2012数据集上评估。

Result: MambaPSA减少2.9%参数量、12.1% FLOPs，CPU推理吞吐量提升17.6%（从17 FPS到20 FPS），精度仅下降0.1 mAP50:95。P4层BiViM插入带来+0.9 mAP50:95的最佳精度增益。

Conclusion: SSM（Mamba）在NMS-free轻量级检测器中替代注意力模块时，能够提供有利的效率-精度权衡，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs), notably Mamba, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to self-attention with linear computational complexity. We investigate the integration of Mamba into YOLO26, the latest non-maximum suppression (NMS)-free object detection framework, by proposing MambaPSA, a lightweight Mamba-based replacement for the C2PSA block at the end of the backbone. To complement this study, we additionally insert a bidirectional Vision Mamba (BiViM) module at the P3, P4, and P5 levels of the neck. Experiments on PASCAL VOC 2007+2012 show that MambaPSA reduces parameters by 2.9%, FLOPs by 12.1%, and improves CPU inference throughput by 17.6% (from 17 to 20 FPS) with negligible accuracy change (-0.1 mAP50:95), while the P4 BiViM placement yields the best accuracy gain (+0.9 mAP50:95). These results suggest that SSMs offer a favorable efficiency-accuracy trade-off when replacing attention-based blocks in NMS-free lightweight detectors.

</details>


### [67] [Lesion Segmentation in Moderate to Severe Traumatic Brain Injury: An nnU-Net Based Approach with Adaptive Normalization in the AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12684)
*Inhwa Son,Gaeun Lee,Sohyeon Sim,Kwang-Hyun Uhm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于nnU-Net框架的msTBI病灶分割方法，核心创新在于引入局限于脑实质的自适应强度归一化策略。在AIMS-TBI 2025挑战赛测试集上，整体Dice系数达0.6305，病灶Dice为0.4805，非病灶组织Dice为0.9324，验证了解剖约束归一化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中度至重度创伤性脑损伤（msTBI）在T1加权MRI上的病灶分割极具挑战性，因为病灶在大小、形状和位置上表现出高度异质性。AIMS-TBI 2025挑战赛旨在推动鲁棒且精准的分割算法研发，本文正是为此提出解决方案。

Method: 采用nnU-Net深度学习框架，并创新性地提出一种自适应强度归一化策略，该策略将归一化操作限制在脑实质区域内进行，以降低受试者间变异并抑制来自非脑结构的伪影干扰。

Result: 在留出测试集上，方法在官方排行榜上取得了极具竞争力的表现：整体Dice系数0.6305，病灶分割Dice 0.4805，非病灶组织Dice 0.9324。

Conclusion: 将解剖约束归一化整合到nnU-Net流程中，是应对msTBI病灶分割复杂性的强有力且有效的策略。高非病灶Dice表明模型区分病灶与非病灶区域具有良好的特异性，而病灶Dice则反映了高度异质病灶检测的固有难度。

Abstract: The segmentation of lesions in Moderate to Severe Traumatic Brain Injury (msTBI) from T1-weighted MRI presents a significant clinical challenge due to the profound heterogeneity of lesion characteristics in terms of size, shape, and location. To address this, the AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge was organized to promote the development of robust and accurate segmentation algorithms. In this paper, we present our deep learning-based solution. Our methodology employs the nnU-Net framework with an adaptive intensity normalization strategy confined to the brain parenchyma, effectively reducing inter-subject variability and mitigating artifacts from non-brain structures. Upon final evaluation on the held-out test set, our method demonstrated highly competitive performance on the official leaderboard, achieving an Overall Dice Coefficient of 0.6305. The model obtained a Dice score of 0.4805 for lesion segmentation and 0.9324 for non-lesion tissue. While the lesion Dice reflects the difficulty of detecting highly heterogeneous lesions, the high non-lesion Dice primarily indicates the model's strong ability to correctly identify non-lesion voxels, demonstrating good specificity in differentiating lesion from non-lesion regions. These results demonstrate that incorporating anatomically constrained normalization within the nnU-Net pipeline is a powerful and effective strategy for tackling the complexities of msTBI lesion segmentation.

</details>


### [68] [Label-Decoupled Style Augmentation for Domain Generalization in Multi-Label Remote Sensing Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12704)
*Alaa Almouradi,Erchan Aptoula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多标签遥感场景分类的标签解耦风格增强框架，通过将风格扰动限定在标签特定区域，解决了现有全局统计增强方法中类别间相互污染的问题，在跨域泛化基准上显著超越已有方法，且几乎不增加参数和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多标签遥感场景分类中的特征统计增强方法（如MixStyle、EFDMix）对通道统计量进行全局扰动，将每张图像视为单一风格，导致一个类别的风格会污染另一个类别的增强。此外，多标签遥感领域的域泛化研究尚属空白，缺乏专门方法和多源基准。

Method: 提出标签解耦增强框架：首先通过可学习模块或梯度类激活映射（Grad-CAM）获取逐标签注意力图，进而计算逐标签特征统计量；然后将这些统计量与共享相同标签的跨域样本进行混合，使用独立的逐标签混合系数；最后通过注意力加权归一化重构特征。三种操作算子和两种注意力来源组合产生六种变体。

Result: 在UCM、AID、DFC15六个共享标签的留一域基准上，三个划分、五个种子的平均结果显示：最佳变体达到71.5% mAP，超过经验风险最小化5.0个百分点，超过最强全局统计基线1.3个百分点；在最难迁移任务上提升高达7.7个百分点。消融实验表明空间注意力和刷新定位图最为关键。框架仅增加最多0.35%参数，不改变推理过程。

Conclusion: 该标签解耦框架为多标签统计基域泛化提供了一种通用、低成本的升级路径，有效缓解了全局风格扰动中的类别污染问题，在多标签遥感场景下展现出显著且稳定的泛化增益。

Abstract: Multi-label classification assigns several co-occurring labels to each aerial scene, yet deployed models often encounter data distributions different from their training. Feature-statistics augmentation such as MixStyle, EFDMix, and correlated style uncertainty improves generalization at low cost but perturbs channel statistics globally, treating each image as a single style; one class can then contaminate the augmentation of another. Domain generalization is understudied for multi-label remote sensing; no prior method or multi-source benchmark targets it. A label-decoupled augmentation framework is therefore proposed, confining style perturbation to label-specific regions. Per-label attention, obtained from a learnable module or from gradient class-activation maps, yields per-label feature statistics; these statistics are mixed with cross-domain samples that share present labels, under independent per-label coefficients, and features are recomposed by attention-weighted normalization. Three operators combined with two attention sources produce six variants, evaluated on a leave-one-domain-out benchmark from multi-label UCM, AID, and DFC15 over six shared labels. Averaged over three splits and five seeds, the best variant attains 71.5% mean average precision, exceeding empirical risk minimization by 5.0 points and the strongest global-statistics baseline by 1.3 points, with the largest gain on the hardest transfer (up to 7.7 points). Ablations indicate that spatial attention and refreshed localization maps are most influential. The framework adds at most 0.35% parameters, leaves inference unchanged, and appears to offer a generic, inexpensive upgrade path for multi-label statistics-based domain generalization. Code is available upon acceptance at https://github.com/Alaa-Almouradi/Style-Augmentation-Upgrade.

</details>


### [69] [Color Pass-Through via Camera-Display Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12746)
*Ruikang Li,Molin Li,Jiarui Wu,Zhe Wei,Pengpeng Liu,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV
