Motivation: 当语言模型面对大量同等有效选项时，它们会如何选择？不同模型之间选择的一致性程度如何？现有研究虽已记录模型收敛现象，但缺乏一个极简、可复现、低成本的工具来系统刻画这种收敛的内部结构。

Method: 设计"一词普查"：31个单轮提示，每个提示指定一个有大量有效单字答案的类别（如"说一个树的名字"），对44个模型各重复4次，无系统提示词。分析采用标准化token的精确匹配，不使用嵌入向量或评判模型，每个模型成本约1美元。核心指标为"答案选择惊奇度"（answer-choice surprisal）：某模型答案在其他所有模型（留一法）汇总答案分布下的平均负对数概率。

Result: 收敛程度极高：31个类别中有7个类别的头号答案占比超80%；但模型间一致性差异逾四倍。人格调优和社区调优模型最发散；最新主流旗舰模型最从众，几乎不产生其他模型未给出的答案。在Claude、GPT、Qwen、Grok四个家族中，从众性随代际递增——但最新Claude和GPT旗舰出现反转，可能是顶层模型重新定位的早期信号。排名对模型列表构成稳健（留一家族ρ=0.985）。与人类类别产出常模相比，在20个共享类别中的18个里，模型比人类更集中。

Conclusion: 一词普查作为一种低成本、高信息量的模型行为测量工具，不仅证实了模型回答的高度收敛，更揭示了收敛背后的结构化差异——模型家族、代际、调优策略均系统性地影响从众程度，最新旗舰模型的反转趋势值得持续关注。

Abstract: When a language model must pick one answer from a large space of equally valid options, which does it pick -- and how often is it the same answer every other model picks? Asked to "pick a word -- any word," 44 models chose "serendipity" 41% of the time. We characterize this convergence with a deliberately minimal instrument: 31 single-turn prompts, each naming a category with many valid one-word answers ("Name a tree."), asked four times per model with no system prompt. Analysis is exact-match on normalized tokens -- no embeddings, no judge -- at about a dollar per model. That models converge is well documented; our contribution is the instrument itself -- the One-Word Census -- and what it reveals about the structure of the convergence. We score each model by answer-choice surprisal: the average $-\log2$ probability of its answers under the pooled answers of all other models, leave-one-out. Convergence is extreme -- in 7 of 31 categories one answer takes over 80% of all answers -- yet conformity varies more than fourfold across models, and the variation is structured. Persona- and community-tuned models are the most divergent; the newest mainline flagships are the most conformist, producing almost no answer no other model gave. Within four lineages (Claude, GPT, Qwen, Grok) conformity rises with each generation -- but reverses for the latest flagship Claude and GPT models, a possible early signal of repositioning at the top tier. Rankings are robust to roster composition (leave-one-family-out rho = 0.985). Against human category-production norms, the field is more concentrated than people in 18 of 20 shared categories. All prompts, transcripts, and code are public.

</details>


### [137] [Knowledgeless Language Models: Suppressing Parametric Recall for Evidence-Grounded Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12831)
*Roi Cohen,Yvan Carré,Nick Lechtenbörger,Hendrik Droste,Lucas Kerschke,Russa Biswas,Gerard de Melo,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Knowledge-Less语言模型(KLLMs)，通过在预训练阶段对命名实体进行匿名化处理，消除实体链接的事实监督信号，从而系统性地将模型从参数化记忆转向基于证据的推理。结果表明KLLMs在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测等任务上表现更优，尤其在检索增强场景下鲁棒性提升显著（相对增益达20-25%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在参数中编码了大量事实知识，但当这些知识过时、不完整或与上下文冲突时会导致不可靠行为。作者旨在探索是否可以通过修改预训练信号，系统性地使模型从参数化回忆转向以证据为基础的推理，从而从根本上改变模型的认识论行为。

Method: 提出Knowledge-Less语言模型(KLLMs)，其核心方法是在预训练语料中对命名实体进行匿名化处理，移除实体链接的事实监督这一主要知识获取通道。通过这种干预，模型被强制减少对参数化知识的依赖。在多个模型规模上训练KLLMs，并在上下文问答、事实验证、幻觉检测以及检索增强场景（含不完美证据）下进行系统评估，同时考察校准指标（ECE、Brier分数、AUROC）和弃权行为。

Result: KLLMs在多个规模上一致优于匹配基线：(1) 闭卷事实回忆能力显著下降；(2) 上下文问答、事实验证、幻觉检测基准上性能提升；(3) 在检索增强且有噪声证据的场景下，相对标准语言模型实现20-25%的相对增益；(4) 校准性能更优，ECE、Brier分数和AUROC均有改善，弃权行为更可靠。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段抑制实体链接监督可诱导模型认识论行为的转变：KLLMs更少依赖参数化知识，更多依赖外部证据，从而在现实条件下展现出更高的可靠性。这表明预训练阶段对知识获取的控制可以作为检索增强和工具系统的补充策略，提供一个对证据更敏感的基础模型。

Abstract: Language models encode substantial factual knowledge in their parameters, which can lead to unreliable behavior when this knowledge is outdated, incomplete, or misaligned with the provided context. In this work, we study whether modifying the pretraining signal can systematically shift models away from parametric recall and toward evidence-grounded reasoning. We introduce Knowledge--''Less'' Language Models (KLLMs), a fundamentally different epistemic training paradigm for LLMs, which are pretrained on corpora in which named entities are anonymized, thereby removing a primary channel for entity-linked factual supervision. This intervention substantially reduces closed-book factual recall, while often improving performance on tasks where relevant information is provided as context. Across multiple model scales, KLLMs consistently outperform matched baselines on contextual question answering, fact verification, and hallucination detection benchmarks. Crucially, in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence, KLLMs show improved robustness and achieve up to 20--25\% relative gains over standard language models. They further exhibit better calibration, with improved ECE, Brier score, and AUROC, as well as more reliable abstention behavior. Our results demonstrate that suppressing entity-linked supervision during pretraining induces a shift in epistemic behavior: KLLMs rely less on parametric knowledge and more on external evidence, leading to improved reliability under realistic conditions. This suggests that pretraining-time control over knowledge acquisition can complement retrieval-augmented and tool-based systems by providing a more evidence-sensitive base model.

</details>


### [138] [Can LLMs Write Reliable Rubrics? A Meta-Evaluation for Experiment Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12835)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi Li,Jiaoyan Chen,Luu Gia Huy,Sophia Ananiadou,Jung-jae Kim,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成的论文复现评估量规进行了系统性元评估，将量规重构为清单格式并测试四种生成设置。结果表明，增强设置在评分对齐上接近人类基线，但LLM量规存在粒度过细、高分偏置和领域适应性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于量规的评估是评估LLM研究智能体开放式输出的有前景方法，尤其在论文复现场景中。然而，构建论文特定量规需要大量专家努力，严重限制了如PaperBench等基准的可扩展性。因此需要探索LLM自动生成量规的可行性及其质量。

Method: 将量规重新构建为清单式格式（checklist-style），在两种骨干模型上设计四种生成设置（基础设置与增强设置）。对生成的量规进行双重元评估：内在评估采用语义相似度衡量，外在评估通过与真值量规的评分对齐程度衡量。进一步分析量规的粒度、评分偏差及领域适应性。

Result: 增强设置显著改善了外在评估的评分对齐，最强设置接近人类基线水平；内在评估的提升相对温和。分析揭示LLM生成的量规往往粒度过细、偏向高分、且对不同论文领域的适应性较差。

Conclusion: LLM生成的量规在论文复现评估中展现出潜力，能够有效降低人工构建成本并提升基准可扩展性，但在细粒度控制、评分校准和跨领域泛化方面仍存在明显局限，需要在未来工作中改进。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising approach for assessing open-ended outputs from LLM-based research agents, particularly in paper reproduction, where direct paper-to-repository comparison is prone to hallucination. However, constructing paper-specific rubrics requires substantial expert effort, limiting the scalability of benchmarks such as PaperBench. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic meta-evaluation of LLM-generated rubrics for paper reproduction. We reformulate rubrics into a checklist-style format and evaluate four generation settings across two backbone models. We meta-evaluate generated rubrics intrinsically by semantic similarity and extrinsically by score alignment with ground-truth rubrics. Our results show that the augmented settings substantially improves downstream evaluation alignment, with the strongest setting approaching the human baseline, while intrinsic gains are more modest. Further analyses reveal that LLM-generated rubrics are often overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less adaptive to paper domains, highlighting both the affordances and limitations.

</details>


### [139] [Evaluating Large Language Models on Misconceptions in Multi-Turn Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12884)
*Monica Munnangi,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ThReadMed-QA多轮医学对话数据集，用于评估LLM在跨越多轮对话中检测和纠正患者错误预设/误解的能力。实验表明，即使前沿模型在首轮能纠正约85%的错误预设，但在后续两轮中性能急剧下降至约50%，主要原因是错误传播，即使在正确上下文中表现也不完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估框架缺乏对多轮对话中误解检测与纠正能力的关注。在真实医患交流中，患者的错误假设可能跨越多轮持续存在或演化，而模型能否在长期对话中可靠地纠正这些误解尚未被充分研究。

Method: 1. 构建ThReadMed-QA数据集：从AskDocs平台收集2,437条医患对话线程，包含8,204对问答；2. 采用基于评分细则的LLM-as-a-Judge框架，对模型识别和纠正误解的能力进行评分；3. 对五款LLM（含GPT-5、Claude-Haiku等）进行多轮评估；4. 进行Oracle分析，用医生真实回复替换模型前置输出，以分离错误传播的影响。

Result: 1. 前沿模型在首轮问题中纠正错误预设的准确率约85%，但两轮跟进后降至约50%；2. Oracle分析表明性能退化主要由错误传播驱动；3. 即使在前置上下文完全正确的情况下，模型表现仍不完美；4. 模型在多轮对话中表现不一致，可能产生不安全的医疗指导。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在多轮医学对话中纠正患者误解的能力随对话轮次增加而显著退化，存在潜在安全隐患。亟需开发能够捕捉多轮行为的评估框架，以推动更可靠、安全的面向患者的医疗对话系统。

Abstract: Patients seeking medical information often ask questions that embed incorrect assumptions or misconceptions. In such cases, safe medical communication requires not only answering the question, but identifying and correcting the underlying false belief. These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.

</details>


### [140] [LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12885)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为评判者（LLM judge）在无参考答案场景下评估开放式模型响应的可靠性。通过校准实验和敏感度实验，作者发现LLM评判者在缺乏参考答案时倾向于对错误答案给予过高评分，而加入参考答案信息可显著改变其判断（某些设置下翻转率高达85%），且这些变化与人类判断基本一致。研究强调了在无参考评估中使用LLM评判者前进行校准的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者越来越多地被用于评估开放式模型响应，且常常在无参考答案（no-reference）的设置下运行。然而，目前缺乏对其在这种评估设置下可靠性的系统性检验，这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 提出一个两阶段分析框架：(a) 校准实验（calibration experiments）：评估LLM评判者对自身所评估任务的知识掌握程度；(b) 敏感度实验（sensitivity experiments）：评估参考答案在提示词中的存在与否及其位置如何影响LLM评判者的评估表现。实验覆盖三种语言，并与人类标注子集进行对比验证。

Result: 在缺乏参考答案的情况下，LLM评判者倾向于对错误答案过度评分（over-credit）；在提示词中加入参考答案信息后，评判者的正确/错误决策翻转率在某些实验设置下高达85%。与人类标注对比表明，这些由参考答案驱动的决策变化总体上与人类判断一致。

Conclusion: 在无参考评估场景中可靠使用LLM评判者之前，必须先用带有参考答案的样本对其进行校准。本文提出的方法论为研究人员和实践者在其他任务中校准LLM评判者提供了操作蓝图。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.

</details>


### [141] [The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12963)
*Yanzhe Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在聚合精度上看似对无关上下文鲁棒，但聚合稳定性掩盖了逐样本的不稳定性：即使是语义无意义的伪词也能显著改变部分样本的预测，这种双面效应受上下文类型、长度、推理计算量和模型发展阶段调制，揭示了聚合指标所隐藏的尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM越来越多地被部署在包含大量不相关上下文的复杂场景中，现有评估主要关注聚合层面的准确率变化，忽视了逐样本层面的预测稳定性。作者旨在揭示聚合鲁棒性背后隐藏的逐样本不稳定性（尾部风险），从而推动面向单样本可靠性的评估范式。

Method: 在受控实验设置下，作者在多个基准数据集的问题前添加不同类型的任务无关上下文（包括随机字符组合生成的语义无意义伪词），对多种最先进LLM进行测试。通过对比聚合准确率变化与逐样本预测翻转情况，系统分析上下文类型、上下文长度、推理阶段计算量（test-time compute）以及模型训练阶段等因素对不稳定性的调制效应。

Result: 1）聚合层面：添加无关上下文后整体准确率变化很小，模型看似鲁棒；2）逐样本层面：即使是纯随机伪词也能在少量样本上显著改变模型预测，且同时存在性能上升和下降两种效应；3）受影响的样本高度模型特异化，不同模型受影响的样本集几乎不重叠；4）不稳定性受上下文类型、上下文长度、推理计算量和模型发展阶段的显著调制。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率掩盖了LLM对无关上下文的逐样本不稳定性，这一尾部风险广泛存在于各类模型和数据集上。研究呼吁评估范式应从聚合指标转向逐样本可靠性评估，以更真实地刻画模型在真实部署场景中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.

</details>


### [142] [PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13027)
*Hongru Cai,Yongqi Li,Ran Wei,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PalmClaw 是一个原生运行于手机端的开源智能体框架，将设备能力封装为带显式参数、结构化结果和清晰执行边界的"设备工具"，相比传统GUI操作方式，任务成功率相对提升11.5%，完成时间减少94.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体主要通过GUI动作（点击、滑动、输入）操作手机，这类方式存在三个核心问题：(1) 形成冗长且依赖界面的操作序列；(2) 无法直接调用设备底层能力；(3) 执行边界难以界定。此外，大多数智能体系统运行在桌面或服务器端，未充分利用移动设备自身的计算环境和数据优势。

Method: 提出 PalmClaw 框架，原生部署于手机上，在设备端直接管理会话、记忆、技能、工具和智能体循环。核心设计是将设备能力抽象为"设备工具（device tools）"，每个工具具备显式参数、结构化返回结果和明确定义的执行边界，使智能体能够直接调用移动设备能力，同时保持每次操作的显式性和可控性。

Result: 在任务成功率上相对最强基线提升11.5%（relative improvement），任务完成时间减少94.9%，且部署负担更低。通过执行轨迹（traces）展示了执行边界如何在实践中发挥作用。

Conclusion: 将设备能力封装为具有清晰边界的设备工具，是一种比传统GUI操作更高效、更可控的移动智能体方案。PalmClaw 验证了在移动端原生部署智能体框架的可行性，为移动设备上的任务自动化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Extracting Bayesian Evidence from Frequentist p-Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12132)
*Frederik Aust,Samuel Pawel,Eric-Jan Wagenmakers*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Jeffreys近似贝叶斯因子（JAB），这是一种可追溯到1930年代的简单方法，它通过仅需p值和有效样本量n_eff，在频率学派的p值与贝叶斯因子之间架起桥梁。作者阐明了JAB的核心假设与边界条件，并在704个已发表的t检验和39个比例比较中验证了其近似效果。核心发现是：p值蕴含的证据强度高度依赖于样本量——传统p值标签仅在n_eff≈8时与贝叶斯因子分级对应；大样本下相同的p值对应更弱的证据，甚至p>.10也可能构成对H_0的中等或强证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p值与贝叶斯因子常被视为哲学和数学上不可兼容的证据度量方式，前者衡量数据与H_0的冲突（"意外程度"），后者衡量H_0与H_1的相对预测精度。研究者希望找到一个能在频率学派和贝叶斯学派客观假设检验之间建立联系的桥梁，从而为p值提供一个样本量敏感的补充工具。

Method: 作者重新梳理了Jeffreys近似贝叶斯因子（JAB）的理论基础，明确其适用于单位信息先验（unit-information prior）下的客观假设检验。JAB仅需p值和有效样本量n_eff即可计算。研究通过704个已发表的t检验和39个比例比较的实证数据，将JAB与客观贝叶斯因子进行对比验证，同时分析了传统p值分级标签（如.001<p<.01为"强意外"）在不同n_eff下对应的贝叶斯因子强度变化，并论证了JAB在可选停止（optional stopping）下依然有效。

Result: （1）JAB在704个t检验和39个比例比较中均能很好地近似客观贝叶斯因子。 （2）传统p值的口头标签（如"强意外"）仅在n_eff≈8时与贝叶斯因子的分级相匹配；随着样本量增大，相同的p值对应的证据强度递减。 （3）在中等到大样本中，p>.10实际上可能构成对零假设H_0的中等甚至强证据。 （4）JAB是一种廉价、样本量敏感的p值补充工具，可从常规报告的统计量中计算，且在可选停止条件下仍然有效。

Conclusion: JAB提供了一种简单实用的方式，将p值转化为贝叶斯因子，使研究者能基于样本量校准对p值的解读。这揭示了仅凭p值而不考虑样本量来推断证据强度可能导致严重误判，尤其在中等或大样本研究中。JAB作为p值的补充，能帮助研究者更恰当地评估零假设的证据。

Abstract: The $p$-value and the Bayes factor are measures of evidence that are often considered to be philosophically and mathematically incompatible: The $p$-value quantifies conflict between data and $H_0$ ("surprise"), whereas the Bayes factor quantifies the relative predictive accuracy of $H_0$ versus $H_1$ ("evidence"). We revisit Jeffreys's Approximate Bayes factor (JAB) -- a simple, largely overlooked approximation dating back to the 1930s -- which connects these two paradigms for objective hypothesis testing of the existence of an effect. Under a unit-information prior the approximation requires only the $p$-value and the effective sample size $n_\text{eff}$. We clarify the core assumptions and boundary conditions for the application of JAB and show across 704 published $t$-tests and 39 comparisons of proportions that JAB approximates objective Bayes factors remarkably well. The connection between $p$-values and JAB has a practical implication: The evidence implied by a $p$-value depends strongly on $n_\text{eff}$. Conventional verbal labels for $p$-values (e.g., "strong surprise" for .001 < $p$ < .01) correspond to similarly graded Bayes factors only around $n_\text{eff} \approx 8$; for larger samples the same $p$-value implies weaker evidence. In moderately sized to large samples, $p > .10$ can amount to moderate or even strong evidence for $H_0$. JAB offers a cheap, sample-size-sensitive supplement to $p$-values, computable from routinely reported statistics, that remains valid even under optional stopping.

</details>


### [144] [Fitting Generalized Linear Mixed-Effects Models using lme4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12184)
*Anna Ly,Rune Haubo Bojesen Christensen,Douglas Bates,Martin Maechler,Benjamin M. Bolker*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: lme4 R包用于拟合广义线性混合模型（GLMM），支持非高斯响应分布和非线性链接函数，通过惩罚迭代重加权最小二乘法估计参数，并利用拉普拉斯近似或自适应Gauss-Hermite求积进行最大似然估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性混合模型（LMM）仅适用于高斯响应分布和线性关系，但许多实际数据（如二项计数、泊松计数）不满足这些假设，因此需要将LMM扩展为GLMM，以处理更广泛的响应分布类型和非线性均值结构。

Method: 采用惩罚迭代重加权最小二乘法（PIRLS）确定随机效应的条件众数、方差-协方差矩阵及固定效应参数；使用拉普拉斯近似（默认）或自适应Gauss-Hermite求积对条件众数分布上的积分进行近似，以获得最大似然估计。

Result: lme4包提供了与R基础GLM一致的标准功能，包括全套访问函数，并支持指数分散族内的用户自定义分布和链接函数。

Conclusion: lme4包为R用户提供了一个统一、灵活且高效的GLMM拟合框架，填补了LMM在非高斯和非线性场景下的不足，成为混合效应模型分析的重要工具。

Abstract: The lme4 R package can be used to fit generalized linear mixed models (GLMMs), which extend the class of linear mixed models (LMMs). The two main extensions provided by GLMMs are (1) allowing for the conditional distribution of the response given the random effects to be non-Gaussian (e.g. binomial, Poisson) and (2) allowing the conditional mean to be a nonlinear function of a linear combination of the fixed and random effect coefficients, via an inverse link function. The conditional mode of the random effects given the observed data, the variance-covariance matrix of the random effects, and the fixed effect parameters are determined using penalized iteratively reweighted least squares. We compute an approximation of the integral over the distributions of the conditional modes to compute the maximum likelihood estimate for a given set of parameters (by default we use the Laplace approximation or, alternatively, the more computationally expensive adaptive Gauss-Hermite quadrature). The package provides all the standard features available for GLMs in base R, including the standard set of accessor functions as well as the possibility of user-specified distributions (within the exponential dispersion family) and link functions.

</details>


### [145] [Nonparametric Multi Change Point Detection for Markov Chains via Adaptive Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12369)
*Imon Banerjee,Jiaqi Lei,Sanjay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对马尔可夫数据序列的非参数离线变点检测算法，通过结合再生马尔可夫链的Rademacher复杂度和基于惩罚的自适应聚类，建立了DKW型不等式，并证明了算法能以接近独立同分布数据的速率准确恢复变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管变点检测在信号处理、语音处理、气候学等领域广泛应用，但对非独立同分布（特别是马尔可夫型）数据集的严格非参数变点检测方法一直缺失。现有方法的理论保证主要针对独立同分布数据，无法有效推广到具有时间依赖性的序列中。

Method: 本文桥接了数理统计的两个分支：（1）利用再生马尔可夫链的Rademacher复杂度最新进展，推导出马尔可夫链经验分布的DKW型不等式；（2）基于该不等式，设计并分析了一种带惩罚的自适应聚类算法来恢复变点位置。

Result: 证明了所提自适应聚类算法能够在马尔可夫序列中准确恢复正确的变点，且恢复速率与独立同分布数据下的已知最优速率基本一致，从而验证了速率的紧性。

Conclusion: 本文为非独立同分布的马尔可夫数据提供了首个具有严格理论保证的非参数变点检测方法，填补了该领域的理论空白，并讨论了计算层面的考量。

Abstract: Offline change point detection tries to detect time points of distribution change in a given data sequence; and is now routinely used in signal processing, speech processing, climatology etc. Despite this broad applicability across economics, computer science, and planetary sciences, rigorous, nonparametric techniques for change point detection with non-independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) datasets has remained elusive. This paper establishes such guarantees by proposing a non-parametric clustering algorithm which can accurately obtain the change points from a given Markovian dataset of length $n$. It does so by bridging together two different components of mathematical statistics; Rademacher complexities of Markov chains, and adaptive clustering via penalisation. Our first result uses recent advances in Rademacher complexities of regenerating Markov chains to derive a Dvoretzky Kiefer Wolfowitz (DKW) type inequality for the empirical distribution of the Markov chain. We then use this to show that an adaptive clustering algorithm recovers the correct change points for a Markovian sequence. We establish the tightness of our rates by showing that they essentially coincide with the best known rates for i.i.d. data. We end the paper by discussing the computational considerations of the problem.

</details>


### [146] [Hierarchical Bayesian inversion using the Karhunen-Loève expansion with analytical eigenpairs of the squared exponential kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12387)
*Tatsuya Shibata,Michael Conrad Koch,Kazunori Fujisawa*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯加权积分特征值问题解析解的Karhunen-Loève展开方法，用于替代传统的数值求解方式。该方法利用平方指数核的解析KL展开，避免了层级贝叶斯推断中超参数更新时对特征值问题的重复数值求解，并通过优化选择高斯权重函数的标准差来降低截断误差，结合HMC实现高效后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在层级贝叶斯反演中，高斯随机场先验的超参数（如标准差和相关长度）需要通过KL展开表示，其基函数依赖于协方差核的积分特征值问题（IEVP）。超参数每次更新都需重新数值求解IEVP，计算代价高昂，亟需一种能够避免重复数值求解的高效替代方案。

Method: 聚焦于平方指数核，利用高斯加权IEVP的解析解构造KL展开，消除超参数更新时对IEVP的重复数值求解。为弥补解析KL展开在均方最优性上的不足，采用基于优化的方法选择高斯权重函数的标准差以降低截断误差。此外，解析KL展开具有闭式导数，支持通过HMC进行高效后验采样。

Result: 一维和二维数值实验表明，所提出的标准差选择策略能为实际应用提供足够精度。在稳态Darcy渗流模型的反演中，该方法成功利用弱信息超先验估计了水力传导系数场。

Conclusion: 基于解析KL展开的层级贝叶斯反演框架，通过消除超参数更新过程中IEVP的重复数值求解，显著降低了计算成本；结合优化选择权重函数标准差与HMC采样，在保持实用精度的同时实现了高效的后验推断。

Abstract: Hierarchical Bayesian inversion with Gaussian random field priors addresses uncertainty in covariance hyperparameters, such as the standard deviation and correlation length. When a Gaussian random field is represented by the Karhunen-Loève (KL) expansion, the basis functions depend on these hyperparameters through an integral eigenvalue problem (IEVP) associated with the covariance kernel. Consequently, the IEVP must be solved repeatedly whenever the hyperparameters are updated, leading to significant computational cost in hierarchical inference. In this paper, we focus on the squared exponential kernel and construct the KL expansion using the analytical solution to a Gaussian-weighted IEVP. This analytical KL expansion offers a computationally efficient alternative to the conventional KL expansion by eliminating the repeated numerical solutions of the IEVP during hyperparameter updates. While the analytical KL expansion is applicable to arbitrary domains and dimensions, it does not have the same mean-square optimality as the conventional KL expansion. To address this limitation, we employ an optimization-based approach that selects the standard deviation of the Gaussian weight function in the IEVP to effectively reduce the truncation error of the KL expansion. Numerical experiments in one- and two-dimensional settings show that this selection strategy provides sufficient accuracy for practical applications. Furthermore, the analytical KL expansion admits closed-form differentiation, enabling efficient posterior sampling via HMC. The proposed framework is applied to Bayesian inversion for a steady Darcy flow model, where the hydraulic conductivity field is successfully estimated using weakly informative hyperpriors.

</details>


### [147] [Does a Developed Comorbidity Index Really Add Value? A Selection-Aware Bootstrap for Post-Selection Concordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12445)
*M. Ehsan Karim*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文揭示了疾病特异性共病指数开发中标准乐观校正方法的结构性缺陷，并提出了一个"选择感知"的自助法来消除因从多个候选模型中选择最佳模型而产生的赢家诅咒偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开发疾病特异性共病指数时，研究者通常构建多个候选模型并报告得分最高的那个，同时声称其相对于Charlson或Elixhauser等固定对照指数具有增量区分价值。然而，标准的乐观校正方法仅将所选模型视为唯一拟合的模型，忽略了"从多个候选中挑选最佳者"这一选择过程本身引入的赢家诅咒偏差，导致置信区间结构性失准，且在真实零假设下会夸大虚假发现率。

Method: 作者提出了一种可直接替代的"选择感知"自助法（selection-aware bootstrap），在每次重抽样中重新执行"从多个候选中选择最佳模型"的完整流程，同时保持对照比较器固定不变。将该方法与标准乐观校正、校准后的交叉验证区间等方法进行比较，采用Uno's C-index作为评估指标。

Result: 在全已知真值模拟中，标准校正的95%覆盖率从仅有一个候选模型时的0.94下降到有100个候选时的0.70，而选择感知区间的覆盖率始终保持在名义水平附近，与校准后的交叉验证区间匹配，且在相同误差率下至少具有同等统计功效。半合成真实调查数据实验进一步证实了该校正方法在实践中的必要性。

Conclusion: 在开发疾病特异性共病指数时，若尝试了多个候选模型构建方式，应报告选择感知区间。当存在大量质量相近的候选模型且每个候选的事件数较少时，该校正最为关键。本文的研究范围仅限于区分度（discrimination）评估。

Abstract: Disease-specific comorbidity indices are routinely developed by building several candidate constructions and reporting the best-scoring one, then claiming it adds discriminative value over a fixed off-the-shelf comparator such as the Charlson or Elixhauser score. We show that the optimism correction in standard use does not make that claim valid. Because it corrects the selected model as if it were the only one ever fit, it omits the winner's-curse term from choosing the best of several candidates; so its confidence interval for the incremental concordance is not merely optimistic in small samples but structurally miscalibrated, and does not shrink as the sample grows. At a true null it inflates false claims of added value above the nominal level, increasingly so as more candidates are screened. We introduce a drop-in selection-aware bootstrap that re-runs the best-of-several selection inside each resample with the comparator held fixed, removing the structural bias. In a fully-known-truth simulation, 95% coverage under the standard correction falls from 0.94 with one candidate to 0.70 with a hundred, while the selection-aware interval holds near nominal; its coverage matches a calibrated cross-validation interval, and at a matched error rate it is at least as powerful. The results hold under Uno's concordance, and a semi-synthetic experiment on real survey data confirms when the correction matters. In practice, if several constructions were tried, report a selection-aware interval, most needed with many similar-quality candidates and few events per candidate. The scope is discrimination only; software and results reproduce every finding.

</details>


### [148] [Symbolic Weak-form Recovery of 2-D Stochastic Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12502)
*Sai Sathvik Gullipalli,Eshwar R A*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 WG-SINDy 估计器，通过协方差形状空间核、岭稳定局部多项式投影、自适应 LASSO/STLSQ 选择、逐分量可行对角 GLS 以及 Cholesky 读出 PSD 投影，从轨迹数据中恢复二维 Itô 生成器。在 29 个合成系统上评估，19 个通过恢复合约，中位漂移误差 0.204，张量误差 0.0397，非退化非对角目标的 a₁₂ 余弦中位数为 0.997。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从轨迹数据恢复二维 Itô 生成器面临三大困难：漂移增量信噪比低、双变量弱设计可能病态、无约束张量估计无法保证正半定性。需要一种能够同时解决这些问题的稳健估计方法。

Method: 提出 WG-SINDy 估计器，组合了以下技术：(1) 协方差形状的空间核；(2) 岭稳定的局部多项式投影；(3) 自适应 LASSO/STLSQ 进行模型选择；(4) 每分量一次样本内可行对角 GLS；(5) 基于 Cholesky 读出的 PSD 投影，并辅以轻度各向同性收缩。实现采用数据依赖的全云平滑器和逐分量样本内可行对角 GLS。

Result: 在 29 个合成二维系统上评估：19 个通过声明恢复合约，8 个作为命名极限保留，2 个为范围综述。在 19 个 PASS 行中，网格中心漂移度量中位数为 0.204，张量误差中位数为 0.0397。6 个具有有限非退化非对角目标的系统中，a₁₂ 余弦中位数达 0.997。正半定性通过构造强制满足。

Conclusion: WG-SINDy 在合成数据上展示了良好的二维 Itô 生成器恢复能力，但结果仅限于样本内采样区域诊断，不能推广到通用或真实数据恢复场景，且不声称有限样本鞅抵消或可行 GLS 效率定理。

Abstract: Recovering two-dimensional Ito generators from trajectory data is difficult because drift increments have low signal-to-noise, bivariate weak designs can be ill-conditioned, and unconstrained tensor estimates need not be positive semidefinite. We study WG-SINDy estimator combining covariance-shaped spatial kernels, a ridge-stabilized local-polynomial projection, adaptive-LASSO/STLSQ selection, one in-sample per-component feasible diagonal GLS pass, and a PSD projection--Cholesky read-out with mild isotropic shrinkage. The released estimator uses a data-dependent full-cloud smoother and one in-sample per-component feasible diagonal GLS pass; accordingly, we do not claim exact finite-sample martingale cancellation or a feasible-GLS efficiency theorem for the reported implementation. We evaluate the estimator on 29 synthetic two-dimensional systems: 19 meet their declared per-system recovery contracts, eight are retained as named limits, and two remain scoped reviews. Across the 19 PASS rows, the median central-grid drift metric is 0.204 and the median tensor error is 0.0397. Among the six systems with a finite, non-degenerate off-diagonal target, the median $a_{12}$ cosine is 0.997. Positive-semidefinite validity is imposed by construction. These results are synthetic, in-sample sampled-region diagnostics and do not establish universal or real-data recovery.

</details>


### [149] [Modification and extension of the Bayesian clinical trial design using external data for single-arm and hybrid-controlled trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12521)
*Wataru Murasaki,Tomohiro Ohigashi,Ryota Ishii,Kazushi Maruo,Masahiko Gosho*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对儿科临床试验中样本量确定的难题，对Psioda和Ibrahim(2019)的贝叶斯框架进行了三项关键改进：(1)将零假设抽样先验重新定义为以零边界为中心的正态分布，(2)在稳健混合先验的第二成分中采用弱信息先验以减轻先验-数据冲突下的偏倚，(3)将方法扩展至混合对照试验。改进方法在维持目标操作特征的同时显著降低了所需样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯外部数据借用框架存在三个核心缺陷：当外部数据显示较大治疗效应时，放宽的I类错误控制失效；抽样先验的支撑集限制可能导致检验效能下降；分析先验的构造可能引入偏倚，且该方法不适用于涉及两组比较的混合对照试验。

Method: 本文提出三项方法学改进：(1)将零假设抽样先验重新定义为以零边界为中心的正态分布，确保贝叶斯I类错误评估的合理性；(2)在稳健混合先验的第二成分中采用弱信息先验替代原有设定，以缓解先验-数据冲突引起的估计偏倚；(3)将整体框架从单组试验扩展至混合对照试验的两组比较场景。通过模拟研究和皮肤红斑狼疮儿科案例验证方法性能。

Result: 模拟研究和儿科案例研究表明，改进方法在维持目标操作特征（如I类错误控制）和控制先验-数据冲突下估计偏倚的同时，相比频率学派方法和原始贝叶斯方法均大幅降低了所需样本量。

Conclusion: 该框架为整合外部信息的临床试验设计提供了一种可靠且高效的方法，尤其适用于患者资源有限的儿科临床试验场景。

Abstract: Limited patient availability complicates sample size determination in pediatric clinical trials. Although Bayesian methods incorporating external data offer a solution, rigorously controlling the type I error rate remains difficult. Psioda and Ibrahim (2019) proposed a simulation-based framework as a practical solution. However, although their framework was designed to relax the type I error control, this relaxation fails when the external data exhibit a large treatment effect, making it difficult to design clinical trials that incorporate external data. Furthermore, restricting the support of sampling priors can cause trial outcomes to fall outside of this support, leading to lower power. Additionally, their analytic prior formulation may induce bias, and their method is not applicable to hybrid-controlled trials involving two-group comparisons. Thus, we propose modifications to both the sampling and analytic prior specifications and extend the framework to hybrid-controlled trials. We redefine the null sampling prior as a normal distribution centered at the null boundary, ensuring a Bayesian type I error evaluation. For the analytic prior, we employ a weakly informative prior for the second component of a robust mixture prior to mitigate bias under prior-data conflict. Furthermore, we extend this methodology to hybrid-controlled trials. Simulation studies and a pediatric case study of cutaneous lupus erythematosus demonstrate that our method substantially reduces the required sample size compared with both frequentist and original Bayesian methods, while maintaining the target operating characteristics and controlling estimation bias under prior-data conflict. This framework provides a reliable and efficient approach for designing clinical trials that incorporate external information.

</details>


### [150] [Accounting for Preferential Sampling Using a Constructed Covariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12809)
*Andreia Monteiro,Isabel Natário,Ivone Figueiredo,Paula Simões*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基于最近邻观测点平均距离的构造协变量来缓解地统计中的优先采样偏差，模拟研究和实际数据验证表明该方法能有效恢复标准地统计推断的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地统计中通常假设采样位置独立于空间过程，但实践中该假设常被违反（如渔业采样倾向于高渔获量区域），这种优先采样会引入显著偏差，影响统计推断的有效性。现有方法多需借助复杂联合建模，本文旨在寻找一种简单易行的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于每个观测点到其最近邻观测点平均距离的构造协变量，将其纳入标准地统计模型（如克里金）中，用以捕捉采样位置对空间变量的随机依赖性。若该协变量充分捕获了依赖结构，则无需诉诸复杂的联合模型即可进行可靠推断。

Result: 广泛的模拟研究涵盖多种采样情景和空间配置，结果表明加入构造协变量能显著降低优先采样的影响。在葡萄牙海洋与大气研究所的渔业上岸量数据和加利西亚铅污染生物监测数据两个实际案例中，方法均展现出良好的实用效果。

Conclusion: 该构造协变量方法为缓解优先采样偏差提供了一种简单有效的工具，使标准地统计方法得以继续使用。同时指出存在实际挑战和局限性，为未来方法学发展指明了方向。

Abstract: In geostatistics, it is commonly assumed that sampling locations are selected independently of the underlying spatial process. In practice, however, this assumption is frequently violated. In fisheries, for example, sampling sites are often chosen to maximize expected catches, creating a stochastic dependence between the abundance process and the sampling design. Such preferential sampling can introduce substantial bias and compromise statistical inference. This study investigates the use of constructed covariates, based on average distances from nearest neighbours observations, that are able to mitigate preferential sampling. The inclusion of such covariate in the geostatistical model might be able to account for the stochastic dependence of sampling locations on the spatial variable. If this inclusion sufficiently captures the dependence, conventional methods of inference may be applied without resorting to more complex models. The proposed methodology is evaluated through an extensive simulation study that explores a variety of sampling scenarios and spatial configurations. Additionally, we demonstrate the practical utility of the approach using two real-world datasets: one on fishery landings provided by the Instituto Português do Mar e da Atmosfera, and another concerning lead pollution biomonitoring in Galicia. Results show that incorporating the constructed covariate can substantially reduce the impact of preferential sampling, enabling reliable inference with standard geostatistical tools. We also discuss practical challenges, limitations, and paths for future methodological development.

</details>


### [151] [Hash-augmented adaptive multilevel splitting Monte Carlo algorithm for accurate estimation of two-sample permutation test p-values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12853)
*Nikita Golikov,Vladimir Sukhov,Gennady Korotkevich,Alexey Sergushichev*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种哈希增强的自适应多层分裂蒙特卡洛算法，用于在双样本置换检验中精确估计任意小的p值，解决了传统蒙特卡洛方法在小p值估计时相对精度差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算置换检验的p值在计算上具有挑战性，尤其对复杂检验统计量而言；而蒙特卡洛采样虽通用，但在估计小p值时相对精度差，影响多重假设检验校正。因此需要一种既能保持通用性又能精确估计小p值的算法。

Method: 提出哈希增强的自适应多层分裂蒙特卡洛算法（hash-augmented adaptive multilevel splitting Monte Carlo）。以Kolmogorov-Smirnov和Mann-Whitney U检验为例，讨论了检验统计量分布的离散性问题及其解决方案。通过与精确算法比较，验证了p值估计的准确性和置信区间的有效性。

Result: 所提算法能够准确估计任意小的p值，并提供有效的置信区间。提供了Python参考实现包hamstest，支持用户自定义统计量的p值估计。

Conclusion: 该算法成功解决了置换检验中小p值精确估计的难题，兼具蒙特卡洛方法的通用性和精确算法的高精度，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Nonparametric permutation tests are widely used for statistical analysis. However, exact computation of test p-values can be algorithmically challenging, particularly for custom tests with complex test statistics. In contrast, Monte Carlo sampling can be easily applied to any test statistic, but it suffers from poor relative accuracy when estimating small p-values, interfering with multiple hypothesis testing correction and leading to other issues. In this work, we present a hash-augmented adaptive multilevel splitting Monte Carlo algorithm that enables accurate estimation of arbitrarily small p-values in two-sample permutation tests. Using the Kolmogorov-Smirnov and the Mann-Whitney U tests as examples, we highlight potential pitfalls related to the discreteness of the test statistic distribution and show how to address them. By comparing with an exact algorithm, we demonstrate the accuracy of the p-value estimates provided by the proposed algorithm and the validity of the associated confidence intervals. We provide a reference implementation of the proposed algorithm in the Python package hamstest, which allows p-value estimation for a user-defined statistic.

</details>


### [152] [Higher-order Spillover Effects Under Partial Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12855)
*Qixiang Xu,Laura Forastiere*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义干扰假设框架，允许个体的潜在结果受网络中更广范围单元（干扰集）处理状态的影响。作者定义了新的因果估计量来量化来自不同网络距离的溢出效应，推导了基于错误干扰集或暴露映射的估计偏差，并开发了Horvitz-Thompson和Hajek估计量及相应的加权回归估计量，最后通过模拟和洪都拉斯母婴健康干预实验验证了方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络因果推断研究在处理溢出效应时，普遍依赖邻域干扰假设（neighborhood interference assumption），即个体的潜在结果仅受一阶邻居处理状态的影响。该假设过于严格，在真实网络场景中往往不成立，因为干预效应可能通过更广泛的网络路径传播。因此需要一种更灵活的广义干扰假设及相应的估计方法。

Method: 作者采用广义干扰假设，允许每个单元有一个"干扰集"（如社区检测算法识别出的社区或有限网络路径可达的单元集）。在该假设下，定义了两个假设的伯努利分布（分别对h阶邻域和干扰集中其余单元使用不同概率），进而定义新的因果估计量。推导了错误干扰集或错误暴露映射函数下的偏差表达式，并开发了Horvitz-Thompson估计量、Hajek估计量及相应的加权回归估计量。

Result: 模拟研究表明，依赖严格干扰假设和暴露映射函数的OLS估计量存在显著偏差，而本文提出的估计量在不同干扰场景和随机图中表现更优。在洪都拉斯两阶段随机试验的应用中，新估计量有效评估了母婴健康干预的溢出效应。

Conclusion: 广义干扰假设比传统的邻域干扰假设更为灵活和现实，基于该假设开发的Horvitz-Thompson和Hajek估计量能够更准确地估计网络中的溢出效应，尤其适用于干扰通过多阶网络路径传播的场景。

Abstract: Interference, under which a unit's outcome is affected by the treatment of other units through network connections, is often present when units interact on a network. When the network of interactions is measured, researchers are often interested in the spillover effect from first-order neighbors. When this is the case, the prevailing approach often involves the neighborhood interference assumption, which is oftentimes overly restrictive. In this paper, we instead rely on a generalized interference assumption, which allows one's potential outcomes to be influenced by the treatment of units from a wider area of the network, referred to as the "interference set". For instance, this can be a community detected through a community detection algorithm, or the set of units that can be reached through a finite network path. Under this assumption, we define new causal estimands to quantify spillover effects from first-order neighbors and, in general, from units at a specific network distance h. We employ two hypothetical Bernoulli distributions with different probabilities for the h-order neighborhood and for the rest of the units in the interference set. We first derive the bias of an approach that relies on a wrong interference set or incorrect exposure mapping function. We then develop new Horvitz-Thompson and Hajek estimators and corresponding weighted regression estimators under the generalized interference assumption. We conduct a series of simulations to assess the bias of OLS estimators -- which rely on restrictive interference assumptions and an exposure mapping function -- , and the performance of our estimators in different interference scenarios and random graphs. We then apply our estimators to a two-stage randomized trial implemented in Honduras to assess a maternal and child health intervention.

</details>


### [153] [Factorial clinical trials in the presence and absence of plausible statistical interactions between treatment factors. A historical review of the methodological literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12991)
*Rebecca E. A. Walwyn,Pritaporn Kingkaew,Alan A. Montgomery,Alexandra Wright-Hughes,Steven G. Gilmour*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 析因试验存在两种思想流派：一是将其视为回答多个单因素问题的"k-in-1"高效设计（假定无交互作用）；二是源自实验设计领域，旨在稳健估计交互作用。本文整合这两派文献，厘清各自的理论依据、治疗对照及其估计量性质，并通过实证案例阐明不同分析策略的影响，最终强调试验者必须明确界定目标与估计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 析因试验的文献中存在两种看似矛盾的指导方针——英国临床试验界主流将析因试验理解为"k-in-1"设计（假设无交互），而实验设计领域则强调析因设计的目的正是为了估计交互作用。这种分歧给试验者带来了困惑和误解，因此有必要整合两派观点以提供清晰指引。

Method: 本文采用综述与论述相结合的方法：(1) 梳理自1935年以来的学术争论脉络；(2) 对比总结两派思想在试验理由、感兴趣的治疗对照及估计量性质上的差异；(3) 通过实证案例展示不同分析方法对结果的影响。

Result: 两派思想的核心分歧在于对交互作用的假设和处理方式不同，这直接决定了估计目标（estimands）的定义、治疗对照的选择以及估计量的统计性质。实证案例表明，不同的分析策略会得出不同的结论。

Conclusion: 试验者必须首先仔细、明确地界定其研究目标，在此基础上才能确定感兴趣的估计目标和治疗对照，而估计量的性质完全取决于这一前置选择。盲目套用某一派的框架而不审视自身目标，将导致分析结果的误用和误读。

Abstract: Factorial trials can be conducted when statistical interactions between two or more treatment factors are not anticipated, but also when they are. A k-in-1 factorial trial answers k single-factor questions with the same number of units as one parallel-group trial if there are no interactions. Literature on k-in-1 factorial trials has dominated trialists understanding of factorial trials in the UK. However, factorial experiments originated from the Design of Experiments field to enable interactions to be robustly estimated. Seemingly conflicting guidance from these literatures poses a source of confusion and misunderstanding for trialists. We bring these literatures together to provide clarity on the arguments that have been used to recommend use of factorial trials in the presence and absence of interactions. We outline motivating examples. We summarise the rationales for using factorial trials, the treatment contrasts of interest, and the properties of their estimators, for the two schools of thought. We describe the debate, going back to 1935, and use an empirical example to illustrate the impact of different analysis approaches. We conclude that it is vital that trialists carefully and clearly specify their objectives. Estimands of interest and treatment contrasts follow, with properties of estimators dictated by this choice.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [OmniPMNet: Bridging discrete and gridded PM10 forecasts via omni-query neural processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11896)
*Shuangshuang He,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OmniPM-Net，一个基于 ConvCNP 的融合模型，将 GNN 站点预报与 CAMS 格点预报统一到共享空间表示中，实现站点和网格双尺度 PM10 预测，在保持站点精度的同时大幅降低格点误差，尤其在高浓度和沙尘事件中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PM10 预报需同时满足站点精度和连续空间场双重需求：CTMs（如 CAMS）提供格点预报但存在局地偏差，GNN 在短时站点预测上表现好但不产生格点输出。亟需一个能融合两类预报、实现站点与网格一致预测的统一框架。

Method: 提出 OmniPM-Net，基于 ConvCNP 架构：(1) 地形感知高斯集卷积将不规则 GNN 站点预报提升至规则网格；(2) 多尺度空间源注意力（SSA）模块将其与 CAMS 格点预报融合；(3) 共享全查询读出解码器在站点或网格单元上生成一致的 108 小时 PM10 预测。

Result: 在全国 1618 个空气质量监测站、2024 全年评估中：(1) 站点级 MAE 21.14 µg/m³，与更强 GNN 基线（22.00）相当；(2) 格点 MAE 较 CAMS 降低 30%；(3) 90 分位 MAE 较 GNN 降 9%、较 CAMS 降 25%；(4) 沙尘事件中分类检测技能提升，且能追踪空间演变轨迹。

Conclusion: OmniPM-Net 成功弥合了站点级 GNN 预报与格点 CTM 预报之间的鸿沟，在统一的共享空间表示下实现了双尺度一致预测，在高污染和沙尘暴场景下优势尤为显著，为业务化 PM10 预报提供了新范式。

Abstract: Forecasting particulate matter (PM10) requires both station-scale accuracy and continuous spatial fields, especially during severe dust storms. Chemical transport models (CTMs) provide gridded forecasts but retain local biases, whereas graph neural networks (GNNs) track monitoring sites well at short lead times but do not produce gridded outputs. Here we present OmniPM-Net, a Convolutional Conditional Neural Process (ConvCNP)-based fusion model that reconciles these two forecast types within a shared spatial representation. A terrain-aware Gaussian set convolution lifts irregular GNN station forecasts onto a regular grid, where a multi-scale Spatial Source Attention (SSA) module blends them with Copernicus Atmosphere Monitoring Service (CAMS) forecasts; a shared omni-query readout then decodes this representation into consistent PM10 predictions at either stations or grid cells over a 108 h horizon. Evaluated across 1,618 air-quality monitoring stations throughout China over the full year of 2024, OmniPM-Net matches the station-level accuracy of the stronger GNN baseline (mean absolute error 21.14 versus 22.00 ug/m3) and reduces the CAMS mean absolute error by 30%, while simultaneously delivering the gridded fields that the discrete GNN cannot. Its clearest gains are in the high-concentration tail, where the 90th-percentile MAE falls by 9% relative to the GNN and 25% relative to CAMS, and during dust episodes, where it improves categorical detection skill while tracking the evolving spatial trajectory.

</details>


### [155] [Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention: Phase-Controlled Delta Memory with Constructive Chunk-WY Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11897)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFDA 提出一种相位控制的块旋转傅里叶注意力机制，将 Kimi Delta Attention 的实数对角衰减推广为复酉旋转，并通过构造性的 chunk-WY 分解实现精确的块间仿射传递，在状态跟踪任务中展示了学习循环记忆的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力用固定循环状态替代 softmax 注意力的 KV 缓存，但压缩限制了精确状态跟踪和长上下文记忆能力。现有 KDA 的实数对角衰减缺乏表达周期性/循环模式的能力，需要引入相位控制来增强记忆表征。

Method: 提出 Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention (SFDA)，核心是将 KDA 的实数对角衰减替换为块旋转傅里叶控制矩阵 Λ_t = diag(α_t ⊙ e^{iθ_t})，状态更新为 S_t = (I − β_t k_t k_t*) Λ_t S_{t-1} + β_t k_t v_t*。主要理论贡献是 A_t = Λ_t − u_t r_t* 形式的构造性 chunk-WY 分解，得到 A_t⋯A_1 = Γ_t − Y_t M_t W_t*，在固定 chunk 内秩增长有界，实现精确仿射块传递及形式化的稳定性和复杂度分析。

Result: 数值上验证了代数分解的正确性；在 toy 状态跟踪实验中，SFDA 成功学习循环记忆，而禁用相位的 KDA 基线表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: SFDA 通过相位控制将 delta 注意力推广到更丰富的循环记忆表征，chunk-WY 分解为精确块传递和复杂度分析提供了理论基础。融合内核和大规模语言模型比较留待未来工作。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces softmax attention's growing KV cache with a fixed recurrent state, but this compression limits exact state tracking and long-context memory. We introduce \emph{Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention} (SFDA), a phase-controlled generalization of Kimi Delta Attention that replaces real diagonal decay with block-rotational Fourier control: \[ S_t=(I-β_t k_tk_t^*)Λ_tS_{t-1}+β_tk_tv_t^*, \qquad Λ_t=\diag(α_t\odot e^{iθ_t}). \] Our main result is a constructive chunk-WY factorization for products \(A_t=Λ_t-u_tr_t^*\), giving \[ A_t\cdots A_1=Γ_t-Y_tM_tW_t^* \] with rank growth bounded inside fixed chunks. This yields an exact affine chunk transfer, formal stability and complexity bounds, and a compact characterization of phase-plus-low-rank memory. We verify the algebra numerically and show in toy state-tracking experiments that SFDA learns cyclic memory where the phase-disabled KDA baseline remains near chance. Fused kernels and large-scale language-model comparisons are left to future work.

</details>


### [156] [Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11928)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhiCalNet 通过架构层面的固定可微标定层，强制网络输出包裹相位而非直接回归深度，从而消除形状先验捷径，将单帧条纹投影测量的物体平均绝对误差降低 3.3 倍，并首次引入共形不确定性量化来定位和滤除误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单帧条纹投影轮廓术(FPP)深度回归网络存在"形状先验捷径"问题：网络倾向于从物体边界而非条纹相位中推测深度，导致性能瓶颈（MAE 14.54 mm），且增加数据量或模型容量均无法消除该捷径，因为优化器的假设空间未改变。

Method: 提出 PhiCalNet：(1) 网络输出包裹相位表示 (sinφ, cosφ)，通过固定的可微标定层将其映射为深度，从架构层面消除形状先验解；(2) 以条纹级次作为辅助输入解决单帧映射的非单射性问题，并证明其对解码误差具有鲁棒性；(3) 引入逐像素共形不确定性量化(conformal UQ)，通过快照不一致性识别并拒绝高误差像素。

Result: PhiCalNet 将物体 MAE 从 14.54 mm 降至 4.46 mm（3.3 倍提升），残差集中在 ±π 包裹不连续处的 0.103% 像素上；三帧扩展版本 MAE 达 1.16 mm。可解释性分析表明相位是最可解码的内部特征；共形 UQ 通过拒绝 5% 高不确定性像素，使 RMSE 降低 64%（基线仅 3.5%）。PINN 软惩罚基线无增益，证明架构选择而非物理信息的引入方式是关键因素。

Conclusion: 通过架构层面的物理约束（固定标定层）而非损失惩罚来消除形状先验捷径，是提升单帧 FPP 精度的有效策略；共形不确定性量化为 FPP 首次提供了可靠的逐像素误差定位手段。

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) networks that regress depth directly can exploit a shape-prior shortcut, recovering depth from object boundaries rather than from fringe phase. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m standoff), the best such UNet baseline plateaus at 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE), and neither more data nor more capacity removes the shortcut, because neither changes the hypothesis space the optimizer searches. We introduce PhiCalNet, which outputs a wrapped-phase representation $(\sinφ, \cosφ)$ and maps it to depth through a fixed differentiable calibration layer, removing the shape-prior solution architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. Because the single-shot mapping is non-injective without fringe order, PhiCalNet takes the fringe order as auxiliary input, an assumption a sensitivity analysis shows tolerates realistic decoding error; a physics-informed (PINN) baseline with the same physics as a soft penalty yields no gain, isolating the architectural choice as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm, its residual confined to 0.103% of pixels at the $\pmπ$ wrap discontinuity, and a three-frame extension reaches 1.16 mm. Two checks agree: interpretability makes phase the most decodable internal feature, and pixel-wise conformal uncertainty quantification, to our knowledge the first for FPP, localizes error at the same discontinuity, where rejecting the top 5% of pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts root-mean-square error by 64% versus 3.5% for the baseline.

</details>


### [157] [Qubit-Efficient Quantum Search for Hyperdimensional Decomposition via Logarithmic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11936)
*Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,Ryozo Masukawa,Raheeb Hassan,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子超维计算（HDC）分解框架，利用对数级超向量编码将量子比特用量从 $O(D)$ 降至 $O(\log D)$，同时保持 $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ 的搜索复杂度，实验显示最高可减少 2000 倍量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超向量分解需在 $N^F$ 候选元组中搜索，计算量随规模急剧增长。现有量子方法虽提供平方加速，但采用 $O(D)$ 量子比特的显式超向量表示，量子比特开销过大，限制了实际可扩展性。因此亟需一种量子比特高效的分解方案。

Method: 提出一种量子比特高效框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 对数超向量编码和对数绑定编码，将超向量的量子表示从 $O(D)$ 压缩至 $O(\log D)$；(2) 可逆超向量查找算子，实现量子电路层面对稠密超向量的高效操控；(3) 改进的 Dürr-Høyer 搜索过程，在降低量子比特数的同时保持 $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ 的查询复杂度。

Result: 实验验证了相似度计算的正确性、可执行规模下的准确分解能力，以及与基于显式 $D$ 量子比特编码的基线相比显著改善的量子比特扩展性——最高实现 2000 倍的量子比特压缩。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持量子搜索加速优势的前提下，大幅降低了 HDC 分解的量子资源需求，为大规模超维计算在实际量子硬件上的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents symbols using high-dimensional hypervectors of dimension $D$. In hypervector decomposition, the objective is to recover $F$ constituent hypervectors, each drawn from a codebook of size $N$, from a bound target hypervector. This requires searching over $N^F$ candidate tuples, making the task computationally prohibitive at scale. Recent quantum approach provides a quadratic search advantage, but typically rely on qubit-inefficient $O(D)$-qubit hypervector representations. We propose a qubit-efficient quantum framework for HDC decomposition that reduces the representation cost to $O(\log D)$. The framework introduces logarithmic hypervector and binding encodings, together with a reversible hypervector lookup operator for circuit-level manipulation of dense hypervectors. Combined with a modified Dürr-Høyer search procedure, the method preserves $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ search complexity while substantially reducing qubit usage. Experimental results validate correct similarity computation, accurate decomposition in executable regimes, and significantly improved qubit scaling over baselines based on explicit $D$-qubit hypervector encodings, achieving up to $2{,}000\times$ fewer qubits.

</details>


### [158] [Mirror Horizon: Viable Path Entropy as a Measure of Bounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11937)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出镜像理论（Mirror Theory），将智能系统的能力定义为其在反复反思下可维持的连贯延续性，并通过可行路径熵（VPE）这一有限预算度量来操作化。在GSM8K数学推理实验中发现，镜像视野（mirror horizon）并非由参数量决定，而是由有界反思协议下的可达验证延续能力决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能评估过度关注系统"表征了什么"（如单次准确率、pass@k），而忽视了系统在反复自我反思下"能维持哪些连贯延续"。作者旨在建立一套理论框架，将能力重新定义为可达的可行延续结构，而非静态输出质量。

Method: 提出可行路径熵（Viable Path Entropy, VPE），将有限预算下的能力分解为两部分：到达可行延续的概率，以及成功展开中验证延续模式的多样性。理论包含四个核心概念：直觉（局部欠定约束）、品味（不变性选择压力）、反思（品味引导的欠定消解）、几何（使未来反思稳定的习得结构）。实验在GSM8K数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-Instruct系列模型，每个问题采样32条展开，设置两个反思视野，token预算从96增至160。

Result: Token预算从96增至160时，验证可达性显著扩大，零可达性降低，验证模式熵增加，平滑VPE提升。关键发现：在160 token下，Qwen2.5-1.5B实现了测试模型中最强的镜像视野，超越了参数更大的Qwen2.5-3B，表明镜像视野并非参数量，而是有界反思协议下的可达验证延续能力。

Conclusion: 镜像理论为智能系统提供了一种度量层面的新解释：能力的本质是可达的可行延续结构，而非单次准确率或pass@k。VPE作为操作化度量有效捕捉了这一结构，实验结果支持了该理论框架的有效性。

Abstract: Mirror Theory proposes that an intelligent system should be studied not only by what it represents, but by what coherent continuations it can sustain under repeated reflection. We make this claim operational through \emph{viable path entropy} (VPE), a finite-budget measure of verified continuation capacity. Given a mirror state, a rollout protocol, a verifier, and a mode map, VPE decomposes bounded capability into two parts: the probability of reaching a viable continuation and the diversity of verified continuation modes reached among successful rollouts. This paper restores the full theoretical scaffold behind the measure: intuition as local underdetermining constraint, taste as invariant-selecting pressure, reflection as taste-guided resolution of underdetermination, and geometry as the learned structure that makes future reflection stable. We then instantiate the theory in language-model reasoning experiments on GSM8K. Across Qwen2.5-Instruct models, 32 sampled rollouts per problem, and two reflection horizons, increasing the token budget from 96 to 160 substantially expands verified reachability, reduces zero-reachability, increases verified-mode entropy, and improves smoothed VPE. At 160 tokens, Qwen2.5-1.5B realizes the strongest mirror horizon among the tested models, even though Qwen2.5-3B has more parameters. This shows that mirror horizon is not parameter count, but accessible verified continuation capacity under a bounded reflection protocol. The result supports Mirror Theory as a measure-level account: capability is the structure of viable continuations made reachable, not merely one-shot accuracy or pass@k.

</details>


### [159] [Mathematics of Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11938)
*Afonso S. Bandeira,Amit Singer,Thomas Strohmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书系统阐述了数据科学的数学基础，涵盖高维现象、矩阵分解、回归与正则化、图与网络、降维技术、优化、分类、深度学习、测度集中、压缩感知和低秩矩阵恢复等核心主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学在过去十年中迅速崛起，但其数学根基分散于统计学、线性代数、优化理论和概率论等多个领域。本书旨在将这些分散的数学工具统一整合，为数据科学研究者和实践者提供严谨而系统的数学基础，弥合理论与应用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 本书采用教科书式的系统组织方式，从高维空间的"诅咒与祝福"出发，逐步展开奇异值分解(SVD)、主成分分析(PCA)、线性回归与正则化、图论与聚类、非线性降维(扩散映射)、随机投影、优化算法、分类方法、深度学习数学导论、图拉普拉斯算子的大样本极限、社区检测、测度集中与高斯分析、矩阵集中不等式、压缩感知与稀疏性、以及低秩矩阵恢复等核心数学工具和理论。

Result: 本书呈现了数据科学数学基础的完整图景：(1)高维空间中的反直觉现象及其对数据分析的深刻影响；(2)SVD/PCA作为核心降维工具的数学原理；(3)正则化回归的理论保证；(4)基于图与谱方法的数据分析框架；(5)随机投影的Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理等理论基础；(6)深度学习的数学表述；(7)压缩感知中稀疏恢复的理论条件；(8)低秩矩阵补全的核范数最小化方法及其恢复保证。

Conclusion: 数据科学的成功实践背后存在一套统一而优美的数学理论体系。对这套体系的深入理解，不仅有助于正确应用现有方法，更是推动领域未来发展的关键。高维几何、谱方法、优化理论和概率集中不等式的交叉融合构成了数据科学的理论支柱。

Abstract: This book is about the mathematical foundations of data science.
  1. Introduction
  2. Curses, Blessings, and Surprises in High Dimensions
  3. Singular Value Decomposition and Principal Component Analysis
  4. Linear Regression and Regularization
  5. Graphs, Networks, and Clustering
  6. Nonlinear Dimension Reduction and Diffusion Maps
  7. Linear Dimension Reduction via Random Projections
  8. Optimization for Data Science
  9. Classification
  10. A Mathematical Introduction  to Deep Learning
  11. Large Sample Limit of Graph Laplacians
  12. Community
  13. Concentration of Measure and Gaussian Analysis
  14. Matrix Concentration Inequalities
  15. Compressive Sensing and Sparsity
  16. Low-Rank Matrix Recovery

</details>


### [160] [CARE-LoRA: Compressed Activation REconstruction for Memory-Efficient LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11940)
*Gengyu Zhang,Haiyin Ran,Zhengbao He,Yuhang Liu,Hanling Tian,Zhehao Huang,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-LoRA提出一种数据感知的压缩激活重建框架，通过利用LoRA的投影结构，在前向传播中用低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活，并在反向传播时通过轻量重建矩阵恢复梯度信号，从而大幅减少LoRA微调中的激活内存占用，同时保持竞争性或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练模型微调面临内存限制，LoRA虽然大幅减少了可训练参数量，但激活值（activations）的存储成为反向传播中剩余的主要内存瓶颈。现有方法未充分针对LoRA特有的投影结构来压缩激活内存。

Method: CARE-LoRA利用LoRA分支天然产生的低秩压缩激活（低秩分支输出）来替代完整输入激活进行存储；在前向传播中以可忽略的额外计算代价计算一个轻量重建矩阵；反向传播时利用该重建矩阵恢复梯度信号，保证LoRA矩阵的完全可训练性。

Result: 在多种模型和下游任务上的广泛实验表明，CARE-LoRA在显著降低整体内存占用的同时，取得了与标准LoRA及代表性LoRA变体竞争甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: CARE-LoRA是一种有效的内存高效LoRA微调方法，通过压缩激活重建机制解决了LoRA微调中激活内存瓶颈问题，在内存效率和模型性能之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: As the scale of large pre-trained models continues to grow, fine-tuning them under limited memory budgets has become increasingly challenging. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), currently one of the most widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, mitigates this challenge by optimizing only low-rank adaptation matrices, thereby greatly reducing the number of trainable parameters. With the parameter overhead substantially reduced, the activations retained for backpropagation have emerged as the primary remaining memory bottleneck during LoRA fine-tuning. To address this, we propose CARE-LoRA, a data-aware Compressed Activation REconstruction framework. By exploiting the inherent projection structure of LoRA, CARE-LoRA replaces the full input activation with the low-rank compressed activation naturally produced by the LoRA branch. It further computes a lightweight reconstruction matrix during the forward pass with negligible additional computation cost, which is used during backpropagation to reconstruct the gradient signal, thereby keeping LoRA matrices fully trainable. Extensive experiments across diverse models and downstream tasks demonstrate that, while substantially reducing the overall memory footprint, CARE-LoRA achieves competitive or even superior performance compared with standard LoRA and representative LoRA variants. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fishandyu/CARE-LoRA .

</details>


### [161] [Are we Merging the Right Models? Impact of Expert Training Duration on Model Merging for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11997)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多任务模型合并中专家模型训练时长对合并质量的影响，发现简单的平均合并方法在过拟合后性能急剧下降，而基于稀疏化的方法在验证最优之后仍能达到最佳性能。作者通过偏差-方差分解对此进行形式化分析，并建议训练时长与合并方法应联合选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多任务模型合并的标准做法是在验证损失最优处合并专家模型，但这一惯例缺乏系统性检验。作者质疑这一假设，旨在揭示训练时长与合并方法之间是否存在交互效应，以及是否不同合并方法有各自最优的训练时长。

Method: 在五个领域（数学、代码、指令跟随、多语言、安全）上分别对三种规模的模型（Qwen 3.5 0.8B、2B、4B）进行微调，保存从最优训练步数的25%到500%的检查点，在每个时间点评估五种合并方法。进一步通过偏差-方差分解进行理论分析，并类比随机森林中高方差个体学习器有利于平均集成的原理来解释实验发现。

Result: 发现了明显的方法依赖模式：简单平均合并在专家过拟合后性能急剧下降；而基于稀疏化的合并方法在验证最优之后仍能达到最佳性能。偏差-方差分解分析表明，平均合并受益于高方差个体学习器，与随机森林的机制类似。

Conclusion: 训练时长和合并方法不应独立选择，而应联合优化。不同合并策略对专家模型的训练程度有不同的偏好，这一发现对多任务模型合并的实践具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging combines separately trained expert models into a single model that handles all tasks without co-training. Standard practice merges experts at their optimal validation loss. We challenge this convention by systematically studying how training duration of domain experts affects the quality of the merged model. We fine-tune experts on five domains (Math, Code, Instruction Following, Multilingual, and Safety) across three model sizes (Qwen 3.5 0.8B, 2B, and 4B), saving checkpoints from 25\% to 500\% of the optimal training steps and evaluating five merging methods at each duration. Our findings reveal a striking method-dependent pattern: simple averaging degrades sharply with overfitting, while sparsification-based methods achieve their best performance well past the validation optimum. We formalize this through bias-variance decomposition analysis, drawing a parallel to random forests where averaging benefits from high-variance individual learners. These results suggest that training duration and merging method should be chosen jointly rather than independently.

</details>


### [162] [How Query Visibility Changes KV-Cache Compression Rankings: A Matched-Budget Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11942)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种已发布的KV缓存压缩方法进行了匹配预算审计，发现查询可见性会显著改变方法排名：在查询无关协议下，大多数方法表现不佳，只有KeyDiff能持续优于简单基线，而最广泛部署的SnapKV甚至不如"保留开头和最近窗口"的简单策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前KV缓存压缩方法的评估普遍采用"查询感知"协议（压缩前将查询附加到上下文），但实际经济部署场景要求压缩是"查询无关"的——即压缩一次文档后回答多个未来问题。这种评估协议与实际部署之间的脱节需要被系统性地审计和揭示。

Method: 作者在三个开源7-9B模型上，对六种已发布的KV缓存压缩方法和三个简单基线进行了匹配预算审计。总计进行了144,300次RULER-8192配对评估和40,800次LongBench评估，并采用50,000次重采样的配对bootstrap方法。除评分规则外，模型、压缩比、实例和解码方式均保持固定。对比了查询感知与查询无关两种协议下的表现差异。

Result: 主要发现有三点：(1) 查询可见性改变排名：在查询无关协议下，五种共享通用注意力后端的方法中仅KeyDiff能持续优于最佳简单基线（36个单元中31个）；最广泛部署的SnapKV平均而言输给了"保留开头和最近窗口"的基线（-0.066）。(2) 两种协议间的方法性能下降与其评分信号中查询的可见程度一致：从SnapKV的Δ=+0.198（查询位于其64 token观察窗口内）到KeyDiff的Δ=+0.011（其评分完全不包含查询项）。(3) 简单基线表现强劲：仅保留首尾token的策略在查询无关场景下常常不逊于甚至优于复杂方法。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩方法的评估协议对其排名有决定性影响。查询感知协议高估了依赖查询信号的方法的实际部署性能，而查询无关协议更贴近真实复用场景。简单基线如"保留开头和最近窗口"在查询无关场景下具有竞争力，研究者应优先采用查询无关协议进行评估，并重视简单基线的作用。

Abstract: KV-cache compression methods are predominantly evaluated with the query appended to the context before compression -- a query-aware protocol. Yet the economic case for a compressed KV cache is reuse: compress a document once, answer many future questions against it. In that deployment, compression must happen query-agnostic -- before any question is seen. We present a matched-budget audit of six published compression methods against three trivial baselines on three open 7-9B models (144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192; 40,800 on LongBench; 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout). Everything is held fixed -- model, compression ratio, instances, decoding -- except the scoring rule. Three findings. (1) Query visibility changes the rankings: under the agnostic protocol, of the five audited methods that share a common attention backend, only KeyDiff beats a best-of-3 trivial baseline consistently (31 of 36 cells), and the most widely deployed method, SnapKV, loses to "keep the start and the recent window" on average (-0.066). (2) The per-method drop between the two protocols is ordered consistently with how visible the question is to each method's scoring signal, legible in its source code: from Delta=+0.198 for SnapKV (the question sits inside its 64-token observation window) down to Delta=+0.011 for KeyDiff (its score contains no query term at all).

</details>


### [163] [Cluster-Weighted EDMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12243)
*Lorenzo Tomaz,Judd Rosenblatt,Flavio Kicis,Thomas B. Jones,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CW-EDMD 通过软聚类联合学习状态空间划分和每个簇的局部 EDMD 算子，利用 EM 目标函数基于几何邻近性和预测残差分配转移样本，从而在 Lorenz、阻尼摆和 Duffing 系统上显著优于单一全局 EDMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 EDMD 使用单一全局 Koopman 算子近似整个状态空间，当不同区域呈现不同局部动力学时效率低下。需要一种能自动发现局部动力学结构的方法，使算子在不同区域各司其职。

Method: CW-EDMD 联合学习软相位空间划分和每个簇的 EDMD 算子。采用 EM 目标函数，根据几何邻近性和预测残差分配每个状态转移样本到不同簇，使聚类专门化于局部 Koopman 模型准确之处，而非仅依据数据密度。

Result: 在 Lorenz、阻尼摆和 Duffing 系统上，跨 36 种配置和 10 个随机种子进行 288 组成对比较：258 组显著误差降低，4 组增加，26 组无差异。一步预测误差中位数降低分别为摆 57 倍、Duffing 2.7 倍、Lorenz 12 倍。

Conclusion: CW-EDMD 通过软聚类与预测残差驱动的样本分配，有效捕捉异构局部动力学，在多系统上一致且显著地优于匹配阶次的全局 EDMD。

Abstract: Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) approximates Koopman operators from data, but a single global operator is inefficient when different state-space regions exhibit distinct local dynamics. We introduce Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD), which jointly learns a soft phase-space partition and a per-cluster EDMD operator. Its Expectation-Maximization (EM) objective assigns each transition based on both geometric proximity and prediction residuals, so clusters specialize where local Koopman models are accurate rather than where the data are dense. On Lorenz, damped pendulum, and Duffing systems, across 36 configurations and 10 seeds, CW-EDMD improves matched-degree EDMD in one-step and 5s-rollout prediction. Across 288 paired comparisons, there are significant error reductions in 258 cases, increases in 4, and no differences in 26. Median one-step error reductions are 57x, 2.7x, and 12x on pendulum, Duffing, and Lorenz, respectively.

</details>


### [164] [BattVAE-GP: Generative Modeling of Long-Horizon Battery Degradation with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11943)
*Raghvender Raghvender,Mahdi Abid,Ferran Brosa Planella,Charles Delacourt,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BattVAE-GP框架：用VAE将DFN/P2D电化学模型生成的电池退化数据编码到二维潜空间，再以稀疏多任务高斯过程在潜空间中对退化轨迹进行插值，实现对未见充放电倍率的退化预测，并提供不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域物理基电池退化模拟计算成本过高，难以对宽广工况范围进行密集探索，需要一种兼具计算效率和不确定性量化的代理建模方法。

Method: 首先用PyBaMM中的DFN/P2D模型生成不同C-rate下的循环退化数据，将数据转化为容量对齐的电压及导数特征；然后用变分自编码器(VAE)将其编码到二维潜空间，使退化轨迹按循环进程和充电协议自然组织；接着在潜空间中以循环次数和C-rate为输入，训练稀疏多任务高斯过程(GP)进行连续插值，同时输出后验不确定性；最后通过冻结的VAE解码器还原电压-容量演化曲线，并通过辅助的潜空间到SOH预测器实现不确定性感知的健康状态估计。

Result: 在协议级留出评估中，潜空间GP能准确恢复未见C-rate轨迹，不确定性行为与训练数据支撑范围一致；在未见内部C-rate查询时，生成的潜轨迹在相邻模拟协议之间保持连贯定位；解码后得到平滑的电压-容量曲线，蒙特卡洛传播给出不确定性感知的SOH估计。

Conclusion: BattVAE-GP框架为长时域退化建模提供了一种计算高效且具备不确定性量化的代理方案，为向更丰富工况扩展及未来仿真-实验融合提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon physics-based simulations of battery degradation provide mechanistic insight but remain computationally expensive, limiting their use for dense exploration of operating conditions over extended cycle life. Here, we propose a hybrid physics-probabilistic learning framework for surrogate modeling of lithium-ion battery degradation trajectories at unseen charging rates. Cycle-resolved degradation data generated with a DFN/P2D electrochemical model in PyBaMM are first transformed into capacity-aligned voltage and derivative features and encoded using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE). The resulting two-dimensional latent space organizes degradation trajectories according to both cycle progression and charging protocol. A sparse multitask Gaussian process (GP) is then trained in this latent space using cycle number and C-rate as input variables, providing continuous interpolation of latent degradation dynamics together with posterior uncertainty estimates. Under protocol-level holdout evaluation, the latent-space GP accurately recovers unseen C-rate trajectories and exhibits uncertainty behavior consistent with the support of the training data. When queried at unseen interior C-rates, the model generates latent trajectories that remain coherently positioned between neighboring simulated protocols. Decoding the GP-predicted latent states through the frozen VAE decoder yields smooth voltage-capacity evolution, while Monte Carlo propagation of the GP latent posterior through an auxiliary latent to State of Health (SOH) predictor provides uncertainty-aware SOH estimates. The proposed BattVAE-GP framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware surrogate for long-horizon degradation modeling, providing a structured basis for extending battery health prediction toward richer operating conditions and future simulation-experiment fusion.

</details>


### [165] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够自然处理复数变量（极坐标表示）的新型玻尔兹曼机——PolarBM及其对数变体LogPolarBM，通过显式建模幅度与相位之间的依赖关系，在音频信号等复数域数据上取得了优于传统模型（包括深度神经网络）的建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法将复数域问题简化为实数域处理，丢失了幅度与相位之间的内在结构关系。作者旨在设计一种能自然捕捉复数信号中幅度-相位物理依赖关系的概率生成模型。

Method: 提出PolarBM，在极坐标下为复数变量定义概率密度函数，使相位显式依赖于幅度。进一步提出LogPolarBM，在对数尺度上建模幅度，导出功率加权非中心复高斯（PW-NCCG）分布，其边缘幅度分布涵盖Rice、Nakagami、非中心卡方等分布。同时引入受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM以用于实际应用。

Result: 实验表明，通过显式建模幅度-相位依赖关系，所提出的RBM在建模精度上优于传统模型（包括深度神经网络）。

Conclusion: PolarBM和LogPolarBM能有效捕捉复数数据的结构信息，不仅适用于音频信号处理，还广泛适用于无线通信、量子力学等涉及复数数据的科学与工程领域。

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [166] [Generalized Distribution-Free Semi-Supervised Learning with Risk Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11947)
*Yushi Hirose,Hiroo Irobe,Takafumi Kanamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无偏风险估计器的广义半监督学习框架，将PNU学习推广至多分类，并在理论上证明了最小可达方差及方差降低对泛化性能的改进，实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习方法依赖分布假设，违反时性能下降；PNU学习虽无需分布假设但仅限于二分类，且其方差最优性不明确。

Method: 通过线性组合各分量风险构建无偏风险估计器，将PNU学习推广至多分类；推导最小可达方差，并建立方差降低与泛化性能之间的泛化界。

Result: 理论证明所提估计器在非对称损失场景下可获得比PNU更低的方差；实验表明所提两种实用SSL方法在二分类和多分类基准上匹配或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 该广义框架克服了PNU学习的二分类限制，通过方差优化理论为半监督学习提供了新的理论支撑和实践方法。

Abstract: Typical semi-supervised learning (SSL) methods rely on distributional assumptions, and their performance degrades when these are violated. While PNU learning, a risk rewriting method, offers a distribution-free alternative, it is restricted to binary classification and its variance optimality remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a generalized framework that constructs unbiased risk estimators using linear combinations of component risks, subsuming PNU learning and extending to multiclass classification. We derive the minimum achievable variance, demonstrating our estimator can attain lower variance than PNU in asymmetric loss scenarios. Furthermore, we establish a generalization bound directly linking this variance reduction to improved learning performance. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce two practical SSL methods that empirically match or outperform existing approaches on binary and multiclass benchmarks.

</details>


### [167] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现标签噪声训练中，深度网络会先学习干净结构再记忆损坏标签，这一转变在最后一层梯度的中心化散布矩阵中留下了谱特征——Fisher有效秩先膨胀后收缩（Fisher Rank Inflation）。损坏样本通过向低能量特征方向注入谱质量来提高有效秩，且可通过一阶留一归因公式识别。该现象在多种模型和数据集上得到验证，峰值秩处损坏样本高度富集，且秩膨胀的起始点可早于测试性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在标签噪声下的训练存在"先学结构、后记忆噪声"的现象，但缺乏一个可量化的谱特征来表征这一过渡。作者希望找到一个能够连接损坏样本富集、损坏程度和从结构学习到记忆过渡的谱信号。

Method: 提出Fisher Rank Inflation（FRI）概念：计算每样本最后一层梯度，构建中心化散布矩阵（类Fisher信息矩阵），通过有效秩度量梯度谱的熵/分散程度。推导一阶留一归因公式，识别哪些样本对秩膨胀贡献最大。在CIFAR-10/100/10N上使用SmallCNN、ResNet18和ViT进行合成损坏和真实损坏实验，追踪训练过程中有效秩的动态轨迹。

Result: 有效秩呈现一致的膨胀-坍缩轨迹，与记忆阶段对齐。峰值秩处，损坏样本在top-100排名中富集（合成损坏：69.2%-96.2%；CIFAR-10N：94.4%±1.9%）。一阶谱归因在卷积模型中与精确留一贡献高度匹配，ViT中也保持富集。峰值有效秩随损坏程度单调递增（干净：28.88±1.95；60%损坏：97.09±1.78）。秩膨胀起始点可早于可观测的测试性能退化。

Conclusion: Fisher Rank Inflation为标签噪声下的学习动态提供了一个统一的谱特征视角：它同时编码了损坏样本的富集程度、噪声严重性以及从结构学习到记忆的过渡时刻，并具有早期预警潜力。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [168] [Scale-Aware Attention for Scarce Neural Data: An RG-Flow Transformer on Sleep-EDF EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11950)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了RG-Flow Transformer（一种引入重正化群归纳偏置的变压器模型）在稀缺脑电图数据上的表现。结果显示，在睡眠分期任务上，RG-Flow与普通Transformer准确率相当（77.3% vs 77.0%），且预期的稀疏数据优势并未出现；但RG-Flow具备独特优势：其学习的异常维度γ能够追踪脑电信号的频谱指数β（R²=0.416），这是普通Transformer无法实现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电场电位呈现无标度特性（功率谱服从1/f^β定律），其非周期指数β与皮层状态（尤其是睡眠深度）密切相关。核心动机是探究：具有重正化群归纳偏置的Transformer是否能借助这一物理先验，在真实、稀缺的脑电图数据上获得性能优势，并赋予模型可解释性。

Method: 提出RG-Flow Transformer，将标准自注意力与一个尺度感知流耦合，该流包含：可学习的异常维度γ、块自旋粗粒化操作、以及熵门控同步桥。使用PhysioNet Sleep-EDF数据集，采用严格的按被试留出交叉验证（防泄漏设计）。实验包括：(i) RG-Flow与参数匹配的普通Transformer及仅保留层次结构的消融模型在5类AASM睡眠分期上的基准对比；(ii) 扫描每被试数据量，寻找稀缺数据下的归纳偏置交叉点；(iii) 检验RG-Flow学到的γ是否能样本外追踪实测频谱指数β。

Result: 在5名被试、5个随机种子的留一被试交叉验证下，RG-Flow与普通Transformer在5类睡眠分期上统计无显著差异（准确率77.3% vs 77.0%，配对p=0.294）。预期的稀缺数据交叉优势未出现——普通Transformer在所有数据受限条件下均数值领先。但模型的可解释性方面差异显著：RG-Flow能样本外恢复连续频谱指数（β恢复R²=0.416），而普通架构不具备此类能力。

Conclusion: RG-Flow Transformer虽然没有带来睡眠分期准确率的提升，但其关键价值在于可解释性：模型能够内在地学习并追踪脑电信号的频谱指数β，实现了从"黑箱"到"物理可解释"的转变。这表明将重正化群先验嵌入深度学习架构，可以在神经信号分析中打开通向机制理解的窗口，即便在行为级分类任务上未见性能增益。

Abstract: Brain field potentials are scale-free: their power spectra follow a $1/f^β$ law whose aperiodic exponent $β$ tracks cortical state, and sleep depth in particular is a shift in $β$. We ask whether a transformer endowed with an explicit renormalization-group (RG) inductive bias -- the RG-Flow Transformer, which couples ordinary self-attention to a scale-aware stream with a learnable anomalous dimension $γ$, block-spin coarse-graining, and an entropy-gated synchronization bridge -- has an advantage over a parameter-matched vanilla transformer on \emph{real, scarce} EEG. Using the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus with a strict leakage-free by-subject hold-out, we (i) benchmark RG-Flow against a param-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class AASM sleep staging, (ii) sweep the per-subject data budget to look for the inductive-bias crossover predicted when data are scarce, and (iii) test whether RG-Flow's learned $γ$ tracks the measured spectral exponent $β$ out-of-sample -- a quantity the vanilla model does not possess. Across $5$ subjects and $5$ seeds under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, RG-Flow and the vanilla transformer are statistically indistinguishable on 5-class staging (77.3\% vs 77.0\% accuracy; paired $p=0.294$), and the predicted scarce-data crossover does not appear: vanilla is numerically ahead at every data-limited budget. What does separate the models is interpretability -- RG-Flow recovers the continuous spectral exponent out-of-sample ($β$-recovery $R^2 = 0.416$), a capability the vanilla architecture has no analogue for.

</details>


### [169] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将Forward-Forward算法中的平方goodness和层间归一化两个启发式选择，置于显式生成模型下重新解释：平方goodness是两零均值总体尺度差异的似然比检验的充分统计量，层间归一化需移除长度而保留逐坐标能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward算法中，将标量goodness定义为平方激活值之和、以及在层间进行激活归一化，这两项设计通常被视为启发式技巧。本文旨在为它们提供一个原则性的生成模型解释，揭示其统计基础并推广到更一般的情形。

Method: 将FF算法置于显式生成模型框架下分析：(1) 将平方goodness解释为两个零均值但尺度不同的总体之间似然比检验的充分统计量，FF阈值即为决策边界；(2) 推广到各向异性情形，得到马氏距离形式的goodness；(3) 推广到重尾分布，得到饱和统计量，其斜率为后验精度（即除法归一化）；(4) 分析层间归一化的作用，说明其须移除长度而保留逐坐标能量，并揭示单位范数归一化导致深度坍塌的原因；(5) 指出成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化后的goodness可消除该问题。

Result: 各向异性推广得到Mahalanobis goodness，普通平方是其各向同性特例；重尾分布下得到饱和统计量，其斜率体现后验精度，表明除法归一化在聚合时具有优势；单位范数归一化会导致深度坍塌，原因在于其移除了长度信息但未正确保留逐坐标能量；白化后的goodness可消除成对目标中的尺度膨胀捷径。

Conclusion: FF算法中的平方goodness和层间归一化并非纯粹的启发式选择，而可从生成模型和似然比检验的角度获得严格统计解释。该视角统一了多种扩展形式，并为改进FF训练策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary.
